=== Orna Gilgamesh Class Guide: Ward Builds, Gear, and Rotations (https://playerecho.com/orna/class-gilgamesh) ===
Gilgamesh class guide for Orna RPG: Ward mechanics, Bastille passive, Spiked Shield III scaling, Hercules vs Ursa celestial variants, gear, followers, and combat rotations.
The Gilgamesh ⁠ is the ★10 end of the warrior classline, succeeding the ★9 Titanguard ⁠ . Everything the class does revolves around Ward : keeping the Ward pool high maximizes both survivability and damage output through the Bastille passive. Unlike the Realmshifter ⁠ , which redlines to 1 HP, or the Heretic ⁠ , which depletes mana, the Gilgamesh ⁠ functions best when its Ward is topped up and maintained.

## Core Mechanics

## Bastille
Bastille is the defining passive of the class. The Gilgamesh ⁠ starts each battle at a reduced Ward percentage, and its offensive power scales as Ward is recovered. Key values:
- +10 turns to Ward duration at battle start
- +5% Ward recovery per turn
- +50% Ward Power , meaning each point of Ward absorbs more incoming damage than on other classes
This recovery rate is what makes the class self-sustaining: you do not need to constantly cast Ward skills to stay effective.

## Siphon Ward
High-tier warrior skills are expensive. The Siphon Ward II passive converts incoming Ward damage directly into mana at a +50% Mana-Ward Recovery rate. As long as the Ward pool holds, enemies effectively refuel your many for skill rotation. This creates a feedback loop in raids: the harder the boss hits your Ward, the more mana you have to sustain Divine Bastion II ⁠ , Ward of Ortanite ⁠ , and Spiked Shield III ⁠ .

## Collateral Damage V
A 15% baseline chance for any physical attack to hit all enemies for an additional 40% damage . Useful in horde dungeons and tower floors, and the foundation of the Ursa variant’s build strategy.

## Defensive Passives
- Steadfast III : 60% base chance to resist negative status conditions. With this passive it is the most status-resistant class in the game.
- Second Chance : 50% chance to survive a killing blow at 1 HP. This is the primary safety net against one-shot spikes in high-Anguish content.
- Guardian : +10% stat bonus when defending territories.

## Skill Progression
The Gilgamesh ⁠ ’s core offensive suite unlocks through level progression up to 240.
Skill | Level | Notes
Guarding Strikes III ⁠ | 225 | Grants Ward turns while dealing damage
Diffuse Ward ⁠ | 225-235 | Converts 30% to 100% of Ward into HP and Mana
Spiked Shield ⁠ | 225 | Entry-tier conversion skill
Spiked Shield II ⁠ | 230 | Mid-tier
Spiked Shield III ⁠ | 240 | Endgame skill: highest damage multipliers and penetration
Diffuse Ward is your emergency button

## Spiked Shield and Damage Scaling
The Physical Attack stat is the primary damage scalar, with Ward as a secondary multiplier:
    Damage ≈ f(Attack) × f(Ward) × Multiplier
A Gilgamesh ⁠ needs both high Ward and competitive Attack to hit damage caps. The Celestial Axe ⁠ and Axe of Gilgamesh ⁠ are the preferred weapons specifically because they provide both stats together.
Spiked Shield III ⁠ has the highest penetration of any warrior skill, allowing it to punch through the massive Defense values of ★10 raid bosses.

## Celestial Variants: Hercules vs Ursa
Celestial classes are sidegrades, not upgrades. Each trades the base Gilgamesh ⁠ ’s status protection for specialized power. Do not switch to either without high-quality immunity gear such as the Ymir Vritra Charm ⁠ .
Celestial classes lose Steadfast III

## Gilgamesh Hercules
Gilgamesh Hercules ⁠ is the Ward-focused path and the best option for clearing tower floors and horde dungeons. Its key addition is Chained Shield ⁠ , an AOE version of Spiked Shield ⁠ . To spam Chained Shield ⁠ efficiently on floors 40-50, a Ward pool between 150,000 and 236,000 is required.
- Best paired with the Cataphract specialization for maximum Ward regeneration.
- Weapon of choice: Celestial Axe ⁠ with Sturdy Feet of Prometheus ⁠ augments for Ward regen.
- Shield of choice: Spiked Greatshield ⁠ or Dangy’s Laptop ⁠ , which provide a hidden +20% bonus to shield-skill damage.

## Gilgamesh Ursa
Gilgamesh Ursa ⁠ shifts toward higher HP and two-handed weapons. It can equip thief-class gear , opening access to high-Attack armor sets like the Black Dragon set that are otherwise locked to the Realmshifter ⁠ line.
The Ursa’s goal is 100% Collateral Damage chance through gear like Arisen Plagued Helmet Diabolus ⁠ , Black Dragon Armor ⁠ ) and Amities. At 100% collateral, single-target skills like Strikes of Ursa ⁠ can spread their full damage to every enemy in the fight, clearing tower floors without needing an AOE skill.

## Equipment

## Weapons and Shields
Item | Notes
Celestial Axe ⁠ | Endgame weapon for Hercules; hosts celestial augments for Ward regen
Axe of Gilgamesh ⁠ | Reliable entry-level ★10 axe; up to 79% Ward when demonforged
Celestial Hammer ⁠ / Labrys ⁠ | Ursa variant; maximizes two-handed passive and collateral stats
Spiked Greatshield ⁠ | Essential for Spiked Shield ⁠ builds; hidden +20% to shield-skill damage
Arisen Shield ⁠ | Highest raw Ward % in the game; standard for non-shield-skill setups
Celestial Axe ⁠ augment note: 5x Sturdy Feet of Prometheus ⁠ (2% Ward boost + 4% Ward recovery per turn each) lets the Gilgamesh ⁠ regenerate tens of thousands of Ward per turn, enabling near-permanent Spiked Shield III ⁠ uptime without manual Ward casting.

## Armor
Slot | Best-in-Slot Options
Helmet | Old Nothren Crown ⁠ for balanced stats and high Ward; Ashen Phoenician Helmet ⁠ ; Fey Yeti Horns ⁠
Chest | Fey Yeti Coat ⁠ for Ward % and water resistance; Jorgunmonder (event)
Legs | Arisen Avalon Knight Leggings ⁠ or Hyperion Greaves ⁠ for HP and Ward capacity
Acc 1 | Ymir Vritra Charm ⁠ for status protection in celestial builds
Acc 2 | Carl’s Ring ⁠ or Ymir Vritra Charm ⁠ to boost Ward and Mana

## Adornments and Amities
- Armor adornments: Jewel of the End ⁠ provide +2% Ward each and are the standard for all armor slots.
- Weapon adornments: Arisen Teardrop ⁠ balance Attack and Ward before you get event-specific drops. Lyncus Paw ⁠ go on celestial axes to increase crit and follower action rates.
- Key Amities: 5% Ward Regen, 40% Crit Damage, 50% longer positive status effects, and the Ward Skill Damage Amity for Spiked Shield ⁠ -focused setups.

## Followers

## Offensive Followers
- Fey Chimera ⁠ : Most common pick for general play and raids. Its Berserk ⁠ buff increases all stats by +50% .The Gilgamesh ⁠ ’s large HP pool handles the self-damage easily.
- Ashen Phoenix ⁠ : Casts Deific Channel ⁠ , which provides a triple-up to all stats. Mandatory for pushing damage caps in high-Anguish raids.

## Ward and Utility Followers
- Arisen Stonewarg ⁠ : The best defensive pet. Frequently casts Ward of Ortanite ⁠ to maintain Ward turns; essential against difficult bosses like Morrigan.
- Orichalcum Golem ⁠ : Budget option for maintaining Ward turns and casting Ward of Mythril ⁠ .
- Great Questing Beast ⁠ : Preferred by some Ursa players for permanent Attack, Dexterity, and Berserk ⁠ buffs.

## Specializations

## Cataphract
The primary spec for Gilgamesh Hercules ⁠ . Grants +12% HP and +3% Ward recovery per turn . Stacked with Bastille’s inherent regen and a Prometheus-augmented Celestial Axe ⁠ . This combination can regenerate over 20% of its total Ward pool per turn , making it effectively unkillable in standard raid environments and allowing continuous Spiked Shield III ⁠ use without manual Ward replenishment.

## Raider
Best for high-speed raiding. Provides Berserk II ⁠ and III, which stack with the follower’s Berserk ⁠ I for a large Attack multiplier. The increased self-damage from Berserk ⁠ is manageable because Siphon Ward keeps mana topped up for healing skills like Osmostrike III ⁠ , letting the Gilgamesh ⁠ sustain an aggressive state longer than other classes.

## Blade of Finesse
Preferred for tower floors. Its Parting Remark ability grants a 45% chance for a free turn after killing an opponent, letting Hercules or Ursa chain AOE attacks through floors with minimal downtime.

## Swashbuckler
An advanced Ursa option. By intentionally reducing physical defense to zero through specialized gear, the Swashbuckler spec grants a large Attack bonus. This may result in a glass cannon build relies on the Ursa’s HP pool and the Second Chance passive to absorb hits while dealing millions of damage per turn.

## Combat Rotations

## Raid Rotation
The Gilgamesh ⁠ follows a “Build, Buff, Blast” cycle in raids against bosses like Arisen Morrigan ⁠ or Apollyon ⁠ :
- Establish Ward : Cast Divine Bastion II ⁠ to route 100% of incoming damage to Ward.
- Primary buffs : Apply Golem’s Fortitude ⁠ , Barrier II ⁠ , Mimic’s Mischief ⁠ , and Wyvern Speed II ⁠ .
- Secondary support : Cast Ward of Ortanite ⁠ to extend Ward duration. Use Deific Channel ⁠ and Diffuse Ward ⁠ if mana is low.
- Damage phase : Spam Spiked Shield III ⁠ as the primary skill. Use Swordplay III ⁠ for Attack/Defense debuffing on high-Defense targets, or Omnistrike III ⁠ , before returning to shield skills.
- Recovery : If Ward drops below 50% , prioritize Ward of Ortanite ⁠ or Mend Wall III ⁠ over attacking.

## Tower Floor Strategy
- Horde management : Use Chained Shield ⁠ (Hercules) or a 100% collateral build (Ursa) to clear groups in 1-2 turns.
- Elite mobs : Switch to single-target Spiked Shield III ⁠ or Strikes of Ursa ⁠ against high-threat targets like the Hydra ⁠ to kill them before they use ultimate abilities.
- Mana conservation on Floor 50+: Use Diffuse Ward III ⁠ after difficult fights to fully replenish HP and Mana, then re-establish Ward turns with a pet cast or Divine Bastion II ⁠ .

## PvP
- Opening : Buff ward on Turn 1 is mandatory against Turn 1 bursts from Realmshifter ⁠ or Deity ⁠ .
- Win condition : Try to use Spiked Shield III ⁠ crit. Against other tanks, use status effects like Stun Dart ⁠ or elemental strikes to chip through the opponent’s Ward before finishing.


=== Heretic Class Guide: Mechanics, Builds, and Gear (https://playerecho.com/orna/class-heretic) ===
Complete Tier 10 Heretic guide: Iconoclast mana mechanics, Ara and Corvus celestials, Sequencer and Maji specs, best-in-slot gear, and the 2025 flask system.
The Heretic ⁠ runs on one principle: the lower your mana, the harder you hit. “Blue-lining” , which means keeping mana near zero without actually running dry, is one of the main mechanics.
This guide covers mechanics, celestial paths, specs, gear, adornments, build archetypes, and the flask system.

## Core mechanics: strength through scarcity

## Iconoclast: the main engine (“Blue-lining”)
Iconoclast activates once your mana drops below 50% of your maximum. From there, every point you lose adds stats. At zero mana , you pick up +100% to all primary stats .
The scaling is linear: for every 1% of mana lost below the 50% threshold, you gain 2% total stats . With 3,000 max mana at 500 current, that’s roughly +72% stats across the board.
Bigger pool means more spells before you hit empty, but slower to reach peak power. Smaller pool peaks fast but risks a complete blackout. Both extremes hurt. Managing the gap is the skill.

## Supporting passives
Passive | Effect | Why it matters
Iconoclast | Up to +100% stats at zero mana | Core damage engine.
Efficacious II | +12% damage on weaknesses, 25% dmg vs. immunities | Improves damage when hitting weaknesses.
Omnimancy II | Periodic full immunity to elemental damage | RNG survivability in multi-element raids.
Life Siphon | 10% heal on hit | My offset Berserk ⁠ self-damage.
Steadfast II | 50% status condition protection | Good for avoiding stuns and petrify in Endless.
Mana Rush | 50% chance for second chance, costs 50% mana | Emergency survival.

## Celestial paths: Ara vs. Corvus
Once you hit ★10 and grind enough Sky Shards from the Towers of Olympia , you ascend to one of two celestial variants. This may genuinely change the rotation.

## Heretic Ara: damage ceiling breaker
Heretic Ara ⁠ is pure damage. Its passive lets spells break the damage cap by up to 400% , which matters against weaker bosses that would otherwise cap your hits.
The whole kit runs on Celestial Sigils (debuffs on enemies that amplify Vesta spell damage). Drop sigils via Sigil Stormflask ⁠ , then fire Vesta spells into them.
Signature Vesta spells: Ara Vesta II ⁠ , Ara Vesta III ⁠ , Ara Vesta IV ⁠ , and Ara Vestaga ⁠ . They deal celestial damage scaled to active sigils.
Even one sigil doubles Ara Vesta II ⁠ power, so it’s the preferred opener right after Sigil Stormflask ⁠ lands.
Ara trades survivability for damage
Heretic Ara ⁠ has reduced HP, Defense, and Resistance multipliers. You hit harder but die faster. Ward investment is not optional.

## Heretic Corvus: evasion and buff lock
Heretic Corvus ⁠ mixes mage damage with thief-class gear access. It’s the better pick for deep Endless dungeons and PvP.
Feature | Detail | Benefit
Equipment Access | Can equip Thief ⁠ gear (armor and accessories). | Evasion and crit options.
Mana Feather | Dodge chance scales up to 60% as mana depletes. | Passive damage avoidance.
Critical Poise | Buffs have only a 3% fade chance on a crit. | Near-permanent buff uptime.
Critical Poise is what makes Corvus work in long fights. Hit 100% crit rate and buffs like Deific Channel ⁠ or Great Meditation ⁠ stop fading. No more watching your T. All Up disappear after one or two turns.

## Specializations

## Maji: stable foundation
Maji gives you the 50% mana cost reduction and Great Meditation II ⁠ , which spikes Magic temporarily. It’s the safest starting point for a fresh ★10.
Cheaper spells mean slower drain, which means Iconoclast activates later. Maji works best in long World raid fights, or in overworld farming where you’re one-shotting mobs and potion efficiency matters.

## Sequencer: glass cannon
Sequencer lets you dual-wield staves, pushing total Magic and adding a chance to Dualcast , meaning the same spell twice per turn for no extra mana.
That second staff also doubles your adornment slots. One staff for crit chance, one for Jewels to cut enemy resistance. The cost is no shield, which hurts Ward badly. You’re betting on armor to cover the gap.

## Bard / Charmer: horde clearer
Mainly for Horde dungeons. Mage’s Dance ⁠ (Bard) and Mage’s Pavane ⁠ (Charmer) deal non-elemental damage that hits every enemy for its full value, no damage split. Elemental resistances don’t apply either, so the matchups that would neuter a mage AOE don’t matter here.

## Cataphract: survival pick
High-floor Tower runs where multi-hit physical attacks punch through your defense. Cataphract adds passive ward regen and supports Transference ⁠ skills to hold ward under pressure. Pull it out when you’re dying a lot.

## Weapons
Staff | Source | Key stats / bonuses | Best for
Arisen Fey Macha Pillar ⁠ | Arisen Morrigan ⁠ | Highest base Magic stat. | Raw DPS in raids.
Arisen Kaladanda ⁠ | Arisen Mammon ⁠ | High Magic + 10 adornment slots. | Ultima ⁠ and crit builds.
Arisen Imagination ⁠ | Arisen Mammon ⁠ | High Magic + T. Berserk ⁠ I chance. | High-risk raiding.
Celestial Archistaff ⁠ | Tower shards | Customizable via Celestial Adornments. | Endgame optimization.
Questing Staff ⁠ | ★9 Glatisant ⁠ / Dragon Roost | Increased EXP, Orn, and Gold gains. | World and dungeon farming.
Arisen Ithra’s Staff ⁠ | Arisen Ithra, the White Knight ⁠ | Built-in crit and high Dexterity. | Entry-level ★10 and crit builds.
Ymir Staff of Zaltys ⁠ | Ymir Amphiptere ⁠ (event) | Passive HP regen + solid Magic. | Sustain-heavy encounters.
Arisen Ithra’s Staff ⁠ is the easiest route into ★10. Built-in crit means less setup to get the class working. Arisen Kaladanda ⁠ tends to win long-term though. Those 10 adornment slots let you stack Jewel of Creation ⁠ or Heretic’s Jewel ⁠ at a depth nothing else matches.

## Armor
Defense and Resistance are thin on this class. Ward does most of the actual work.
Head: Old Nothren Crown ⁠ is the reliable default, with massive ward and defensive stats even at base rarity. Fey Menja’s Hood ⁠ if you need more adornment slots or foresight for PvP.
Body: Heretic’s Robe ⁠ has the highest Magic boost but increases mana costs and has zero ward. Glass cannon only. For tower pushing or deep raids, take Fey Yeti Coat ⁠ or Arisen Rift Armor ⁠ for the ward.
Legs: Baldr Boots ⁠ for HP and ward. Fallen Sky Shoes ⁠ split the difference between Magic and ward, useful when you drop Heretic’s Robe ⁠ .
Off-hand: Arisen Shield ⁠ gives the best ward percentage for any non-Sequencer build, or go with Arisen Morrigan’s Scroll ⁠ for the manaflask charge rate.

## Adornments

## Weapon adornments
- Ferocious Bull’s Eye ⁠ : Roughly 4% crit chance each. Stack until you hit 100% .
- Heretic’s Jewel ⁠ : Chance to inflict Resistance Down on hit. Vital against ★10 raid bosses like Morrigan.
- Ashen Ruby ⁠ : HP recovery on hit. Keeps you from dying at low mana or low HP without chugging potions.
- Blackened Eye of Kerberos ⁠ : Boost pet action rate. Required if you’re running Ashen Phoenix ⁠ for Deific Channel ⁠ .
- Jewel of Creation ⁠ : Dragon events only. Best-in-slot all-around for most pieces when you can get them.

## Armor adornments
Ward percentage first. Jewel of Ward ⁠ (1% or 2%) is the standard from chests and world drops. For endgame gear, Jewel of the End ⁠ from the Apollyon ⁠ raid cover Defense, Resistance, and Ward together with no trade-offs.

## The flask system
Flasks are a separate action menu. They don’t cost spell slots or mana and charge up by hitting weaknesses with elemental spells.
Flask | Effect | Application
Banishment Flask ⁠ | Removes summons from the field. | Counters Summoner ⁠ and Grand Summoner ⁠ in PvP.
Manaseep Flask ⁠ | Inflicts Manaseep on self, draining mana each turn. | Automated mana drain for Iconoclast.
Sigil Stormflask ⁠ | Applies random celestial sigils to multiple enemies. | Preps targets for Ara Vesta ⁠ spells.
Bloodloss Flask ⁠ | Deals mana damage to self (approx. 2/3 of pool). | Instant mana drop for full Iconoclast power.
Mana-surge Flask ⁠ | AOE damage with a chance for an extra turn. | Fast dungeon and tower clearing.
The Bloodloss Flask ⁠ fixes something that was a real problem before: there was no fast way to dump mana in short fights like PvP or World bosses. Pop it on turn one and you’re at Iconoclast active immediately. On Corvus, Mana Feather evasion kicks in at the same time.
Flasks do not cost mana or spell slots
Charging flasks by hitting weaknesses also activates Efficacious II’s damage bonus, so you gain flask charge and deal more damage at the same time.

## Builds

## Raid specialist
Survive the opener, stack buffs, then unload through Ara Vesta ⁠ or Ultima ⁠ .
- Class / Spec: Heretic Ara ⁠ with Sequencer (burst) or Maji (stability)
- Weapons: Dual-wield Arisen Fey Macha Pillar ⁠ and Arisen Kaladanda ⁠
- Armor: Old Nothren Crown ⁠ , Fey Yeti Coat ⁠ , Baldr Boots ⁠
- Pet: Ashen Phoenix ⁠ (required for Deific Channel ⁠ of not used as a skill)
Rotation:
- Buffs: Ward of Ortanite ⁠ , Divine Bastion II ⁠ , Golem’s Fortitude ⁠ , Wyrm’s Song ⁠ , Mimic’s Mischief ⁠
- Debuffs: Outwill ⁠ (Resistance down), Sigil Stormflask ⁠ (apply sigils)
- Damage: Ara Vesta II ⁠ primary, Ultima ⁠ to break the damage cap, Multi-element III
- Sustain: Transference III ⁠ (ward), Drain III ⁠ (HP recovery)

## Dungeon specialist (Endless)
Endless runs are the main Orn and EXP farm at ★10. Corvus’s dodge and buff lock are what let you push past the floors that kill other builds.
- Class / Spec: Heretic Corvus ⁠ with Maji or Sequencer
- Gear focus: Evasion and Ward. Baldr ⁠ gear or Thief ⁠ -gear hybrids
- Pet: Chimera ⁠ for Berserk ⁠ buffs on lower floors, swap to Ashen Phoenix ⁠ higher up
- Key tactic: Keep 100% crit rate to trigger Critical Poise. Use Stun Dart ⁠ or Sleep ⁠ on dangerous physical enemies, since one hit from them can end a run.

## World farming
Overworld bosses and standard dungeons where speed and bonus scaling matter more than damage ceiling.
- Class / Spec: Heretic ⁠ with Maji or Bard
- Weapons: 2 Questing Staff ⁠ (Demonforged or Godforged) for Orn, EXP, and Gold multipliers
- Armor: Lost Helmet ⁠ and Court Jester’s Outfit ⁠ for resource multipliers
- Accessories: 2x Band of Gods ⁠ or Arisen Monster Tome ⁠
- Spells: Magic Strikes III ⁠ , Sorrow III ⁠ , Mage’s Dance ⁠ for AOE clearing


=== Orna Realmshifter Class Guide: Redline Builds, Gear, and Progression (https://playerecho.com/orna/class-realmshifter) ===
Guide to the Realmshifter classline: Redlining to 1 HP, Ward mechanics, specializations, weapon tiers, and builds for dungeons, raids, towers, world farm, and endless.
The Realmshifter ⁠ classline is built around deliberately dropping to 1 HP to trigger the Resurgence passive, which grants a +100% boost to all stats . Your Ward pool is your real health bar. Everything in the class, from gear choices to pet selection, follows from that single rule.

## Redline: the core mechanic
Ward of Ortanite ⁠ and Divine Bastion II ⁠ both provide 100% Ward absorption : incoming physical damage hits Ward, not HP. Keep your Ward pool topped up and your 1 HP is untouchable by physical attacks.
Status effects bypass Ward
Poison, Blight, Rot, and other DoT damage hits HP directly, ignoring Ward entirely. Use immunity gear rather than relying on Ward to block them: Briny Pendant ⁠ , Ring of Annwn ⁠ , Smelly Shoes ⁠ , Beguiled Mystic Katar X ⁠ .
Two passives protect your 1 HP:
- High Tenacity : Prevents Berserk ⁠ damage from killing you, leaving exactly 1 HP.
- Mystic Feather : 60% dodge chance at low HP.

## Getting to redline
- Berserk ⁠ : Primary method. High Tenacity ensures Berserk ⁠ stops at 1 HP.
- Double Edge ⁠ : 10% self-damage per use. High Tenacity does not protect against this.
- Quad Edge ⁠ : 40% self-damage. No Tenacity protection.

## Important Buffs
Defensive:
- Ward of Ortanite ⁠
- Divine Bastion II ⁠
- Golem’s Fortitude ⁠
- Barrier II ⁠
Offensive:
- Mimic’s Mischief ⁠ / Bear’s Might ⁠
- Gait of Gunnr ⁠
- Warcry ⁠
- Wyrm’s Song ⁠
- Deific Channel ⁠

## Crit Build
This is one of the easiest build to run early on.
Make sure you have enough base crit chance to ensure 100%. Using Realm Strikes II ⁠ for a 20% crit bonus and Wyrm’s Song ⁠ for another 20%, so you only need 60% crit in your stat screen for a guaranteed crit hit.
Try to get two +40% crit Amities and use them with oracle specialization to maximize damage.
Celestial Bow ⁠ with Steady Hands of Prometheus ⁠ augments also increase crit damage by 50% and will increase your output a lot.
Don’t forget about your offensive/defensive buffs.

## Recharge and Bloodshift
Recharge returns 25% HP and Mana on every crit. One crit at 1 HP and you’re out of redline.
Two ways to fix this:
- Use non-crit skills during setup to avoid triggering Recharge before you commit.
- Use Bloodshift ⁠ (available at level 235 ) to prevent all HP healing and stay locked at 1 HP regardless of crits.

## Avidity
Avidity II is the Shadowmancy passive, giving a 15% chance for an extra turn after each hit. Maximum proc rate is 30% , achievable through Beastfelled Garb ⁠ and adornments such as Lyncus’ Bristle ⁠ and Monoceros Essence ⁠ .

## Specializations
Spec | Primary use
Blade of Finesse | AoE dungeon sweeps; paired with Dorado or Corvus
Zweifencer | Early dungeon sweeps on base Realmshifter ⁠
Raider | Raids; Berserk III ⁠
Swashbuckler | World farm one-shot builds
Cataphract | Tower Slice builds
Oracle | Endless farming and world farm

## Weapons

## Farming weapons

## Classlines
The Realmshifter ⁠ unlocks at level 225 and has two Celestial branches:
- Realmshifter Dorado ⁠ : Warrior-type. Opens warrior gear slots. Preferred for Chained Shield ⁠ builds, AoE dungeon sweeps, and tower climbing.
- Realmshifter Corvus ⁠ : Thief-type. Preferred for high-damage raid builds and Corvus Endless runs. Its Poise and Critical Chain mechanics sustain consistent crit output across long fights.
In Endless, Beowulf / Bestla ⁠ is the buffing class. Its Call of Brynhild ⁠ passive speeds up follower buff casting during setup.

## Builds
At level 225, before targeting event gear, use the strongest available non-event items. Priority accessories are Briny Pendant ⁠ and Ring of Annwn ⁠ in every build.
Slot | Item
Weapon | Arisen Ithra’s Blade ⁠ or Arisen Gandring ⁠
Off-hand | Arisen Shield ⁠
Head | Old Nothren Crown ⁠ or Avalon Crown ⁠
Body | Fey Yeti Coat ⁠
Acc 1 | Briny Pendant ⁠
Acc 2 | Ring of Annwn ⁠
Add Ward adorns (Jewels of Creation) as soon as you can to help you survive tougher enemies.
Try to get your luck bonus as high as possible why still being able to finish the dungeon.
Good Weapons may be Arisen Gandring ⁠ for its off-hand skill, or Questing Bow ⁠ .
For Amor you may want to use Mightiest Mimic Head ⁠ for its massive bonuses.
When doing a crit build be sure to use 40% crit damage Amities. For non crit build or Corvus/Dorado build use mana regen Amity or potions to sustain.
Always cast cast Ward of Ortanite ⁠ / Divine Bastion ⁠ and offensive buffs, then redline with Berserk ⁠ .
As raids are not effected by luck you can just use your best damage gear.
You may want to go with Celestial Bow ⁠ / Finesse’s Katana ⁠ as a weapon and for armor using high ward pieces to survive.
Ymir Brilliant Feathers ⁠ may be your goto weapon here, as it’s off-hand skill will kill stuff fast.
Slice builds target 100% crit on turn one with max avidity.
For weapon use Venin ⁠ or Arisen Deathbringer ⁠ .
Good farming armor will be Mightiest Mimic Head ⁠ , Court Jester’s Outfit ⁠ , Coral Boots ⁠ and 2x Band of Gods ⁠ .
Endless has three phases: a buffing phase ( Beowulf ⁠ ), prep (setting up dungeons and Ward turns), and the actual run. Every gear slot in the run phase should contribute to orns or exp.

=== Orna Summoner Class Guide: Unlock, Builds, and Endgame Paths (https://playerecho.com/orna/class-summoner) ===
Guide to the Summoner classline: unlock via Horus, mid-game XP gear, Blood Pact math, Ward builds, and the Celestial GSA vs GSH endgame split.
The Summoner ⁠ classline deals damage through autonomous summons whose output is entirely independent of your ATT and MAG stats. You exist to survive, and keep the summons alive. That changes everything about gearing, from ★7 to the Celestial endgame.

## Unlock
The Attuner ⁠ (★7) costs no Orns but you need to complete the Horus quest chain. Horus is a winged NPC that spawns randomly in the overworld. In Heroes of Aethric he spawns in Myrkheim.
The chain has two parts:
- Locate and kill specific Arisen monsters.
- Collect the full Attuning set ( Attuning Hood ⁠ , Attuning Robe ⁠ , Attuning Shoes ⁠ ). These drop exclusively from Arena wins.
Deliver the full set to Horus and the Attuner ⁠ unlocks for free.
The following classes like Grand Attuner ⁠ costs orns as for other class lines.

## Tier 7 to 9: gear philosophy
Your stats do not affect summon damage
From ★7 Attuner ⁠ to the ★9 Summoner ⁠ you can ignore combat stats entirely. Equip whatever maximizes XP, Orns, and item drop rate.
Best-in-slot progression gear:
- Weapon: Questing Staff ⁠
- Head: Mightiest Mimic Head ⁠ / Mimic Head ⁠
- Chest: Court Jester’s Outfit ⁠
- Legs: Jolly Stockings ⁠
- Accessories: 2x Band of Gods ⁠ or any kind of Monster Tome ⁠
Apply Buffs ( EXP Potion ⁠ , Dowsing Rod ⁠ , Lucky Coin ⁠ , Lucky Silver Coin ⁠ ) before doing any activity. You may also apply Shrines of Wisdom and Shrines of Luck if you have them available. Farm Horde and Boss Horde dungeons and hope for your summons to absorb all aggression. You can also kill world bosses.
Your character is fragile during this phase. Use Ward of Ortanite ⁠ and Golem’s Fortitude ⁠ to establish a Ward buffer early.
Summon Cockatrice ⁠ petrifies high-threat targets and stops them from using area-of-effect attacks that bypass summon aggro and instantly kill a low-HP player.

## Specializations
Spec | Primary use | Trade-off
Benefactor | Passive summon output | reduced base HP/DEF/RES
Sequencer | Blood Pact ⁠ builds, dual-wield staves | Requires Quickcast/Dualcast skill investment
Maji | Horde farming, mana efficiency | Pairs well with Heretic’s Robe ⁠
Charmer | Endless dungeons, Sonatas on summons | Low burst, high sustained passive output
Oracle | Amity content | Niche outside Amity hunts
Benefactor is the standard pick from ★7 through mid-endgame. The penalty applies to base HP, DEF, and RES only, not to gear bonuses. It also unlocks Summon Ancient Jinn ⁠ , the spec’s signature AoE summon.

## Summon roster
Summon output follows an additive formula:
    Total multiplier = (1 + passive bonuses + gear bonuses + spec bonuses) × (1 + Ascension Level / 100)
The Grand Summoner ⁠ base class alone adds +200% to the additive pool. High-tier Benefactor contributes roughly +130% more. Because the pool is already so large, a gear piece offering +15–20% summon stats provides a tiny proportional gain in the endgame. Build tanky instead. Ward gives more return on investment.

## Good Summons
Summon | Notes
Summon Buggane ⁠ | Your goto summon in most cases
Summon Bone Mammoth ⁠ | Tanky and good damage
Summon Giant Panda ⁠ | AoE damage
Summon Great Hydra ⁠
Summon Troll ⁠
Summon Chimera ⁠ | Inflicts Toxic, Blight, and Rot
Summon Dark Pegasus ⁠
Summon Ancient Dragon ⁠ | Dragonblight passive doubles dragon damage
Summon Mighty Griffin ⁠ | Tanky summon
Summon Guivre ⁠
Summon Gazer ⁠
Summon Very Scary Skeleton ⁠

## Specialist summons
Summon Phaethon ⁠ : unlocks at level 230 for Grand Summoner Auriga ⁠ and applies multiple buffs to your party.
Summon Ancient Jinn ⁠ (Benefactor only): AoE summon whose damage scales down with enemy count. Approximate output: ~15,000 vs 1 target, ~10,000–11,000 vs 2, ~7,000–8,500 vs 3, and ~5,000 each vs 5.
Summon Cockatrice ⁠ : high petrification rate. Locks down threats that would otherwise bypass summon aggro.

## Blood Pact
Blood Pact ⁠ converts a percentage of your active summons’ combined HP into magic damage. Two factors matter equally: the total HP pool of your summons, and your MAG stat. Buff MAG with Jinn’s Talent ⁠ and Wyvern Speed ⁠ before casting. Skipping buffs cuts output regardless of how large the HP pool is.
Spell | Cast time
Blood Pact ⁠ | 1 turn
Blood Pact II ⁠ | 1 turn
Blood Pact III ⁠ | 2 turns
Blood Pact II ⁠ is the standard pick. Blood Pact III ⁠ doubles the multiplier but costs two turns, making Blood Pact II ⁠ more action-efficient in sustained PvE.
The HP pool is everything. Summon Great Hydra ⁠ has roughly 3.6× the base HP of Summon Colossus ⁠ . Five Great Hydras active means roughly 3.6× more damage from the same Pact cast. Summon Kelpie ⁠ or Summon Bone Mammoth ⁠ is the other strong HP-pool pick.
Life Pact II ⁠ restores summon HP at the cost of mana, so repeated Pact casts stay at full output.

## Ward mechanics
Ward is the primary defensive layer in endgame play. It absorbs incoming damage before it reaches HP, and the right combination of spells, gear, and Augments scales it high enough to absorb almost everything.
Avoid summon-stat gear in the endgame

## Ward spells
Spell | Effect
Ward of Ortanite ⁠ | 85% damage mitigation
Divine Bastion ⁠ / Divine Bastion II ⁠ | 100% damage mitigation
Transference III ⁠ | Ward recovery combined with an attack

## Ward gear
- Arisen Shield ⁠ (best-in-slot ward percentage)
- Fey Yeti Coat ⁠

## Grand Summoner: GSA vs GSH
The Grand Summoner ⁠ unlocks at level 225 and includes Paired Essence IV, which auto-casts your first 2 summons to the start of each battle. After that, the two Celestial paths split completely.

## Grand Summoner Auriga (GSA)
Grand Summoner Auriga ⁠ should be your first unlock as it has more use cases as Hydrus. GSA opens warrior gear slots and adds +220% summon stat. Its Bastion III passive gives your summons 25% chance to intercept a hit targeting you. But Pact spells do 70% less damage.
Celestial Axe ⁠ at level 20 provides 60% Ward. With Augments, GSA sustains a Ward pool of 100,000–150,000 , enough to absorb most incoming damage.
GSA Augments:
Augment | Effect
Sturdy Feet of Eos ⁠ | Reduce turns needed to cast summon spells
Sturdy Feet of Prometheus ⁠ | +2% max Ward plus +4% per turn Ward recovery
Lungs of Themis ⁠ | Start each battle with Ward already active
Heart of Themis ⁠ | 3% max HP and 3% max Mana recovered when Ward takes damage
Shoulders of Eos ⁠ | +7% summon protection chance
Heart of Eos ⁠ | +12% summon stats

## Grand Summoner Hydrus (GSH)
Grand Summoner Hydrus ⁠ is more niche than Auriga. This class can use thief gear and Charon Pact ⁠ generates summons automatically each turn. It can generate a lot of damage in raids by sacrificing his summons.
Weapons: Celestial Staff ⁠ main hand. Off-hand: Arisen Charon’s Staff ⁠ or Nekromancer’s Staff ⁠ .
Buff your magic as high as possible and use Summon Dead ⁠ to create a lot of summons to sacrifice for Hydrus Pact II ⁠ , your main damage skill.
GSH Augments:
Augment | Effect
Arms of Eos ⁠ | 1.2x pact effectiveness

## Endgame content

## Towers of Olympia
GSA is the preferred path here. The Ward depth from Augments and a leveled Celestial Axe ⁠ handles most tower floors, and Bastion III provides passive protection by your summons.

## World Raids
GSH clears faster. Setup: Sequencer spec, Heretic’s Robe ⁠ and Fallen Sky Shoes ⁠ , pre-buffed with Deific Channel ⁠ , Jinn’s Talent ⁠ , and Gait of Snotra ⁠ . Then use Summon Dead ⁠ to create a lot of summons to sacrifice for Hydrus Pact II ⁠ .

## Endless Dungeons
You may need specific setup here to get your summons buffed as good as possible. For buffing your summons you need Arisen Ophion Banner ⁠ which has a chance to mirror each buff you give yourself to your summons. Try to use Sleep Dart ⁠ and/or Stun Dart ⁠ for tougher enemies.

## How to play
Build tanky and let the summons carry the damage, especially in earlier tiers. Prioritize survival: high HP, ward, and defensive stats.
Before summoning each fight, cast your buffs first: Ward of Ortanite ⁠ , Golem’s Fortitude ⁠ , and Barrier II ⁠ . Getting those up before to summon will result in you taken less damage if a hit gets through.
After that place your summons on the field and try to stay alive.
For Hydrus with Hydrus Pact II ⁠ , the rotation changes. Sacrifice three summons to hit max pact stacks, then use Summon Dead ⁠ to refill. Repeating that sacrifice loop is what drives ★11 damage output.

=== Codexing Guide: Complete Every Bestiary Entry (https://playerecho.com/orna/codex-guide) ===
How to fill the Codex in Orna and Hero of Aethric: elements, status immunities, turn-burning, hard monsters, and seasonal content.
Filling the Codex is how you earn the Master Researcher achievement. This guide covers what each entry requires, how to document each cluster efficiently, and how to handle the entries that will actually give you trouble.

## What the Codex Tracks
There are multiple sections that have to be completed to before the monster turns yellow in the interface, which means you have completed its codex. Any monster still white means you missed something.
- Drops : all possible loot the entity can drop
- Skills : every action in the monster can use
- Immunities : which damage type the monster is immune to
- Weaknesses : which damage type the monster is weak to
- Resistances : which damage type the monster is resisting
- Status immunities : which debuffs the monster is immune to
The official Codex isn’t showing all of those stats, but you can look at https://codex.fqegg.top/ ⁠ which shows all required stats.

## Elemental Affinities
The game has nine damage types . Hit the monster with each type to reveal its resistance: weakness (more damage), resistance (less damage), immunity (zero), or sometimes even absorption (it heals from the hit).
Element | Primary research skills
Physical | Basic Attack, Omnistrike ⁠
Water | Icestrike ⁠ , Frost ⁠ , Glacier ⁠
Fire | Firestrike ⁠ , Flame ⁠ , Inferno ⁠
Lightning | Lightningstrike ⁠ , Spark ⁠ , Fulmination ⁠
Earthen | Earthstrike ⁠ , Tremor ⁠ , Cataclysm ⁠
Holy | Holystrike ⁠ , Purify ⁠
Dark | Darkstrike ⁠ , Shadow ⁠
Dragon | Dragonstrike ⁠ , Beaststrike ⁠
Arcane | Arcanus ⁠ , Arcana ⁠
At Level 245+ , the spell Ultima ⁠ hits all nine elements in a single cast and gives you the full affinity profile in one turn. Nothing else comes close for endgame researchers.

## Skill Documentation
Every monster is cycling through its actions. To document a skill, you need to watch the monster use it. Rare skills can take a long time to appear, so you extend the fight deliberately.
Some enemy skills only activate past a threshold:
- Heal ⁠ and Great Heal ⁠ typically do not fire until HP drops below ~10%
- Mend Wall ⁠ fires when Ward drops below ~10%
Use Damage-over-Time effects like Poison ⁠ or Miasma ⁠ to chip HP down slowly. The most common mistake is critting through the threshold and killing the monster before it reacts.

## Status Immunities
Attempt each debuff and get either a successful application or an “immune” message in the battle log.
Status | Primary skill | Alternative source
Poisoned | Miasma ⁠ , Poison ⁠ | Great Mimic’s Mischief ⁠
Bleeding | Coup de Grace ⁠ , Bleed ⁠ | Great Mimic’s Mischief ⁠
Burning | Firestrike III ⁠ , Burn ⁠ | Fire Adornments, Jewel of Fira ⁠
Frozen | Icestrike III ⁠ , Freeze ⁠ | Ice Adornments, Jewel of Arcana ⁠
Paralyzed | Lightningstrike III ⁠ | Lightning Adornments, Jewel of Arcana ⁠
Rot | Earthstrike III ⁠ | Earth Adornments, Jewel of Arcana ⁠
Blind | Smoke Bomb ⁠ , Flash ⁠ | Sands of Aaru ⁠
Asleep | Sleep Dart ⁠ | Arisen Witch’s Scroll ⁠
Stunned | Stun Dart ⁠ , Cyclone ⁠ | Jewel of Arcana ⁠
Blight | Miasma ⁠ , Blightstrike ⁠ | Great Mimic’s Mischief ⁠
Petrified | Cockatrice Breath ⁠ (follower) | Gargoyle ⁠ (follower)
Att↓ | Mimic’s Mischief ⁠ | Blast ⁠
Mag↓ | Mimic’s Mischief ⁠ | Blast ⁠
Def↓ | Mimic’s Mischief ⁠ | Exploit ⁠ , Onslaught ⁠
Res↓ | Mimic’s Mischief ⁠ | Magic Sword ⁠
Items like Naggeneen’s Belt ⁠ and Arisen Genius ⁠ give passive bonuses to status application rates, which helps at the margins.

## Petrification
Petrification is the hardest status to log. The Gargoyle ⁠ and the Cockatrice ⁠ follower both sit at low application chance. Budget for dozens of turns of doing nothing while you wait.

## Great Mimic’s Mischief
Great Mimic’s Mischief ⁠ is learned by the Archdruid ⁠ class at level 160 . It is the fastest way to tick off multiple statuses at once. The downside: it can apply those same debuffs back to you.
Equip the Arisen Arch-Gadget ⁠ or the Ring of Day ⁠ first. Both grant immunity to the Defense and Resistance drops the skill inflicts, so you can spam it without hurting yourself.

## Codexing Low-Tier Monsters
At ★10 (Level 225+) , your base stats can one-shot low-tier monsters before you collect any data. You need to deliberately nerf yourself by ditching the weapon and cast low-tier magic spells.
Gait abilities push it further. Gait of Snotra ⁠ and Gait of Hildr ⁠ drop a specific offensive stat when you need to really pull your punches.

## Follower AI and Codex Integrity
Followers and pets can contribute to Codex flags. One exception matters: summons from the Attuner ⁠ and Summoner ⁠ classes do not count . Interactions triggered by summons are not credited to your entry.
Aggression is the other issue. High-damage followers like Greater Yokai ⁠ and Carman ⁠ are good at landing status effects but can kill a monster before it cycles through its rare skills.
Summoner and Attuner players
Summons from your class abilities do not register Codex interactions. Only your active follower or pet counts!

## Hard-to-Codex Entities

## The Enlightened Trio
The Enlightened Guardian ⁠ , Enlightened Mage ⁠ , and Enlightened Acolyte ⁠ show up in Mystic dungeons and Shaded Caves. Enter the dungeon, find one, exit, and swap classes/skills. You can re-enter the same floor has no cooldown as long as it is unfinished.
You need to see specific buffs and the Second Chance ability.

## Monsters using Mend Wall
Demon Knight ⁠ , Living Armor ⁠ , Orichalcum Golem ⁠ , Adamantine Knight ⁠ , Living Armor ⁠ , Mythril Armor ⁠ requires triggering Mend Wall ⁠ . That only fires when Ward drops to roughly 10–15% without HP running out first.
Attack with no weapon equipped shave the Ward bar down slowly. The tricky part is not accidentally killing it through HP damage in the process.

## Seasonal Entities
A chunk of the Codex can only be filled during seasonal events. Miss the window and you wait until it comes back.

## Wild Hunts
The Winter Wild Hunts are the biggest seasonal obligation for completionists. Four waves, each dropping different entities:
Wave | Theme
Wave 1 | Phoenixrise, Beastfelled, Plight of Apollyon
Wave 2 | Thronemakers, Riftfall, Ragnarok
Wave 3 | Fallen Heroes of Avalon, Wyrmhunt, The Hallowed
Wave 4 | The Crimson Festival, Of Giants and Titans
Every wave matters if you are going for Master Researcher. Many of these entries have no other source.


=== Orna Daily and Weekly Checklist: Routines by Time and Type (https://playerecho.com/orna/daily-weekly-checklist) ===
Daily and weekly activity checklist for Orna and Heroes of Aethric, with 10/30/60-minute routines and beginner-to-endgame breakdowns.
Quick reference for what to do each day and week in Orna and Heroes of Aethric, organized by time budget and player type.

## What resets and when
Activity | Frequency
Login Reward | Daily
3 daily quests | Daily
Shop restocks | Every 24, 48, or 72 hours per shop
Kingdom War (24-hour duration) | Starts on demand (do at least 1 HP damage to each)
Astraltree | Weekly
Weekly quests | Weekly

## Core daily checklist
- Log in and collect your Login Rewards
- Check your 3 daily quests and note which activities overlap so you can stack them
- Check shops for Monster Remains , Dowsing Rod ⁠ , Arena Token ⁠ , Affinity Candle ⁠ or needed materials
- Collect Diluted Mnemonic ⁠ from Oracle Temple (once build or unlocked)
- Try to run at least one dungeon
- Tag every open kingdom raid for at least 1 damage to enter the loot pool
- Check kingdom quests for any you can knock out during a normal farming session
- Participate in the kingdom war and kingdom gauntlet if you are assigned to it
Stacking daily quests multiplies your orns

## Routines by time budget

## 10 minutes
- Collect Login Rewards
- Check all 3 daily quests. Start the easiest one if it fits your current location
- Tag any open kingdom raids for 1 damage each
- Collect Diluted Mnemonic ⁠ from Oracle Temple
- Check at least one shop for useful items

## 30 minutes
- Full 10-minute checklist above
- Run 1 or 2 dungeons (hard mode if you can clear them - doubles kill rewards, guarantees Superior+ quality on completion drops)
- Complete 1 or 2 daily quests, prioritizing ones that overlap

## 60 minutes
- Full 30-minute checklist above
- Clear multiple dungeon types to maximize reward variety
- Stack quests with your farming route
- Do a memory hunt for Proof of Remembrance ⁠ and Amities
- Visit a Monument dungeon and collect Proofs of Monument
- Check Territory Control settlements if you have the Conqueror’s Guild Hall built

## Routines by player type

## Beginner (Tier 1 to Tier 4)
Daily quests are your main source of XP and orns. Complete all 3 when possible. Run dungeons to farm gear, and prioritize boss dungeons for better drop quality. Skip kingdom features until you join a kingdom. The Astraltree still works, so collect Astralseed ⁠ even if you are not engaging with the tree itself yet. Log in everyday to build your login streak.

## Mid-game (Tier 5 to Tier 8)
Start running dungeons consistently. Join a kingdom and tag every raid, even if you only deal 1 damage. Begin stacking daily quests when multiple quests point at the same activity. Choose kingdom quests that line up with your current farming zone to complete both at once.

## Endgame (Tier 9 to Tier 10)
Kingdom Raids against Apollyon ⁠ and The Morrigan ⁠ are near-mandatory at this stage. Towers of Olympia becomes available at ★9: it is longform content requiring a full session per attempt, and losing a run costs half your collected Tower Shards (you can exit early and keep everything if you are sure you will die). Plan your sessions around that. Boosters, quests, and exploration zone farming all stack together, so the more you overlap them the faster progression moves.

## Casual player
Log in every day even if you play for only a minute. The daily login reward is passive and requires nothing other than opening the app. The weekly quest only requires 9 completed daily quests out of a possible 21 across the week, so you can miss roughly 12 without failing it. Pick the daily quests you enjoy and skip the rest.

## Weekly checklist
- Finish at least 9 daily quests to complete the weekly “9 dailies” quest
- Complete the longer weekly quest (check your quest log for this week’s target monster or raid type)
- Spend Astralseeds on the Constellation branch you are currently working toward
- Check the Astraltree after its weekly reset for newly unlocked paths
- Run Towers of Olympia if you are ★9+ and have time for a full session
- Upgrade gear at the Blacksmith if you have materials ready

## What to skip without guilt
- Towers of Olympia: ★9+ only and high stakes. You need to finish higher floors to get a good Tower Shards income. Skip entirely until you have time for a proper attempt and the gear to support it.
- Memory hunts: GPS-optional. You will not miss critical progression by skipping them.
- Hard mode dungeons: Only skip if you are under-geared. Run standard first, upgrade at the Blacksmith, then return to hard mode when you can clear it cleanly.
- Kingdom wars: You only participate when assigned. When not selected, there is nothing to do.
- Leaderboard climbing: Worth pursuing only if competitive play is your goal. It has no effect on personal gear progression. The most efficient path is boss dungeons and quests.

## Avoiding burnout
The daily structure is lighter than it looks. The weekly quest needs only 9 of 21 possible daily completions . Miss a day entirely, and you still only need to hit 9 across the remaining 6 days.
The minimum daily action is one tap: open the app, collect the Login Rewards, close it. That single action keeps your login streak running.
Each dungeon and gauntlet type has its own individual cooldown, which naturally limits how much you can grind in a single session. Work with the cooldown structure rather than against it: do what is available, then step away. The game is designed for short daily bursts.


=== Orna Dungeon Guide: Modes, Keys, Cooldowns, and Godforging (https://playerecho.com/orna/dungeons) ===
How dungeons and the gauntlet work in Orna and Hero of Aethric: tiers, modes, key costs, cooldowns, and Godforging.
Dungeons unlock at level 50 (★3) . This covers the full system: gauntlet vs dungeon, all four modes, key costs, cooldown reduction, themed dungeons, and Godforging.

## Gauntlet vs Dungeon
The menu Gauntlet opens from anywhere. World-map Dungeons are fixed buildings you travel to in Orna, or permanent fixtures in specific regions in Hero of Aethric.
Every dungeon and your Gauntlet have separate cooldowns. Load up on keys and chain multiple runs back-to-back by moving through the world.
In Orna , you can use Explorer’s Waystones to locate all dungeons nearby, then route through them to chain completions. Each world dungeon resets on its own timer, independent of the menu gauntlet.
In Hero of Aethric , dungeons are permanent fixtures in specific regions. The daily dungeon count is capped by how many regions you travel through. World bosses in HoA respawn instantly, which partly compensates for the lower dungeon density.

## Dungeon Types
Beyond standard dungeons, several specialized types exist:
- Themed dungeons : Focus on a specific monster family. Themed dungeons always scale to your current tier and cannot be set lower when running solo. Dragon Roosts : dragon enemies Battlegrounds : Lyonesse and Nothren foes Beast Dens : Key-positive even in Horde Mode because beast bosses drop keys at high rates Portals : Good for farming Summoning Scroll ⁠
- Dragon Roosts : dragon enemies
- Battlegrounds : Lyonesse and Nothren foes
- Beast Dens : Key-positive even in Horde Mode because beast bosses drop keys at high rates
- Portals : Good for farming Summoning Scroll ⁠
- Monuments : Event and god-based instances found on the world map. Weekly location resets.

## Dungeon Tiers
Before entering some dungeons you can pick a tier from the dropdown. It defaults to your current tier if you skip it.
At ★4 (level 75) , you unlock the ability to set standard dungeons below your tier. This is the most reliable way to farm quest mobs that stop spawning in the overworld as you level. Themed dungeons like the Underworld Portal are locked to your current tier when running solo.
Tier | Level | Approx. floors | Themed dungeon
★1 | 1-24 | 5 | Mushroom of Perilous Visitation
★2 | 25-49 | 5-8 | -
★3 | 50-74 | 10 | Mystic Cave / Shaded Cave
★4 | 75-99 | 12 | Great Desert Tomb
★5 | 100-124 | 12-15 | Beast Den / Dragon Roost
★6 | 125-149 | 15 | Chaos Portal
★7 | 150-174 | 18 | (multi-boss spawns begin)
★8 | 175-199 | 20 | Battlegrounds
★9 | 200-224 | 22 | Underworld Portal
★10 | 225-249 | 25 | Valley of the Gods
★5 is the first real step up: more floors and more bosses than any ★1-4 run. Each floor is locked to a specific mob tier, but which monster fills the slot is random.
Dungeons are the go-to for biome farming, including water enemies, without waiting on conditions.

## Boss Floors
Any boss floor can roll a boss from a lower tier.
The final boss rule: excluding ★10-11, the final boss will always be within 2 tiers of the dungeon tier. A ★9 dungeon can end on a ★7, ★8, or ★9 boss.

## Dungeon Modes
Four modifiers change difficulty, cost, and rewards. They stack.

## Boss Mode
Replaces all monsters with bosses. Boss kills inside dungeons pay about 25% of their world-map value , but the volume of bosses makes this the best mode for gear and key farming. At ★7 , key drops from bosses typically exceed the 5-key entry cost. From that point, Boss Mode is self-sustaining.

## Horde Mode
Spawns 4-5 enemies simultaneously on every floor. Good for materials and event mob farming because it multiplies encounters per key. The catch: all enemies attack before your next turn. Glass cannon builds get punished here. You need solid Defense, Resistance, and Ward to survive consistently.

## Hard Mode
Enemies hit harder and items (like health potions) are disabled during combat . In return:
- Orns and XP pay out from bosses at approximately 50% of world values and 100% from normal mobs.
- End-of-dungeon quest rewards are guaranteed Superior quality or higher
If you’re hunting an Ornate Old Nothren Crown ⁠ or a better Band of Gods ⁠ , this is the mode. Otherwise, standard Boss Mode gives a better key-to-loot ratio.

## Endless Mode
No floor cap. You go until your build breaks. Rewards start suppressed and scale with depth:
Floors | Reward rate
1-80 | ~25% of base
81-160 | ~125% of base
161-240 | ~225% of base
Past floor 80, the XP scaling makes Endless the best exp source at ★10 for builds that can sustain across hundreds of floors. Past floor 100, enemy stats scale high enough that multi-hit skills deal 0 damage without sufficient armor and resistance penetration. High-penetration skills and debuffs like Exploit ⁠ and Outwill ⁠ become necessary to go deep.

## Key Costs
Skeleton Keys (formerly Gauntlet Keys) are the entry currency.
The costs depend on the number and which modes you enable for the dungeon. If you are low on keys try not to enable all modifiers.
The key-positive threshold is ★7 to ★8. Below that, you spend keys faster than you earn them. At ★8, consistent Boss Mode farming stockpiles hundreds or thousands of keys over time.
You can choose which specific events to include in a run from the UI. If an event adds mobs you’re not farming, exclude it and get more other spawns.

## Cooldown Reduction
The Unfelled Concord ⁠ (★10 accessory) cuts the 6-hour cooldown:
- 1x Unfelled Concord ⁠ : 6h drops to 3 hours
- 2x Unfelled Concord ⁠ : drops to 1 hour
The stacking is non-linear. The second Concord doesn’t halve the remaining 3 hours; it stacks to a flat 1-hour timer. At 1 hour per run, that’s 24 gauntlet runs per day from stationary play.
Cooldown reduction applies to world-map dungeons too, not just the menu gauntlet. Each world dungeon has its own timer. A Keep or Fortress built in your Origin Town adds a permanent dungeon with an independent cooldown.

## Completion Rewards
Clearing a dungeon adds bonus drops on top of what mobs dropped. These bonuses don’t exist in Endless Mode , which has no finish line.
Every clear gives extra Orns and random materials . You can also pull:
- Random Unknown Origin items that can roll elemental resistances
- Quest items from previously completed story or NPC quests, at improved quality. This is how you get a second Band of Gods ⁠ or more Aldon’s Hat ⁠ .
Hard Mode guarantees a minimum Superior quality drop from the completion screen.

## Godforging in Dungeons
Godforging takes gear from level 12 (Demonforged) to level 13 , the max. Outside of hunting Mammon ⁠ in the open world, ★10 and ★11 dungeons or towers are where it happens.
Kill an Arisen Superboss (yellow glowing aura) with at least one demonforged item in your loadout, and you have a chance to get a godforged version of it.

## Tier 10 vs Tier 11
★10 has 13 bosses total, 5 of which are Arisen Superbosses . They only spawn on the final floor .
★11 places Arisen Superbosses on floor 16 and floor 25 . Characters at ★11, level 250 get double the Godforging chances per run versus ★10.

## Finding Keys
The main free sources:
- Great Mimic ⁠ and world bosses : Kill them on sight.
- Quests : Some hand out keys directly.
- Kingdom Raid bosses : From Starlord ⁠ onward, keys drop even if you’re below their tier. Deal at least 1 damage to qualify.
- Beast Dens : Key-positive even in Horde Mode. Beast bosses drop keys at high rates.
- World bosses via Monster Remains ⁠ : One of the most efficient non-dungeon key sources.
- Scroll of Chaos ⁠ (Orna only) : Using a Scroll of Chaos ⁠ while traveling fast as a passenger increases spawn rates for key-dropping bosses.
Join a kingdom early

## Common Mistakes
Hard Mode by default is a key sink. Hard Boss dungeons cost up to 25 keys for specialized instances. Unless you’re specifically hunting an Ornate quest reward like Band of Gods ⁠ , regular Boss Mode gives a better key-to-loot ratio.
Horde Mode without a defensive build. 4-5 enemies all attack before your next turn. A pure-offense build gets deleted fast. Sort out Defense, Resistance, and Ward first.
Advancing tiers with no key buffer. Entering ★8 with empty pockets and outdated gear means you’ll struggle to farm the new tier’s bosses.
Endless Mode past floor 100 needs penetration. Enemy Defense and Resistance scale high enough that multi-hit skills deal 0 damage without armor and resistance penetration. High-penetration skills and debuffs like Exploit ⁠ and Outwill ⁠ are required to go deep.

## Quick Reference
Goal | Mode | Tier
New tier gear | Boss | Current
Key farming | Boss | ★7 or ★8
Exp (solo) | Endless | Current
Orn farming | Boss + Hard, Endless | Current
Quest mobs | Normal | Target tier
Event loot | Horde + Event | Current
Material farming | Horde | Current


=== Event: Beastfelled 2026 - Eriq of Annwn Trials, Every Boss, and the Lyncus Super Raid (https://playerecho.com/orna/event-beastfelled) ===
Beastfelled is the trial event. Eight challenges from Eriq of Annwn, three raids, a craftable dungeon, and Lyncus.
“Sword or no sword, staff or no staff. To don a suit of armor or leave ourselves vulnerable. Trials await us.”
You get 8 trials , 3 raids , 4 followers , and a dungeon key that turns the whole event into farmable content.

## Quest line
Find Eriq of Annwn to start. He runs the Beastfelling, a series of collective trials the people of Annwn put themselves through, and he is fairly blunt about expecting travelers to fail them.
The trials are split into three named sets that climb in tier: Kerberos ⁠ , Monoceros ⁠ , then Manticore ⁠ . Each one gates the next, and each one asks you to fight with something deliberately taken away.

## How to actually clear them
Here are some tips for the quests to finish:
Trial | What trips people up
Kerberos: Trial I | The finishing blow has to register as weak damage. Check the codex for the weakness
Kerberos: Trial II | Every hit in the battle must register as weak, not just the last one
Monoceros: Trial I | Final blows must be critical hits. Stack crit from spells, skills, adornments and gear
Monoceros: Trial II | Your opponent must act 6 times and you take zero damage, DoTs included, then win
Manticore: Trial I | Codex-hunt a boss in your tier and beat it with the weapon slot empty
Manticore: Trial II | Edit your Dungeon loadout to strip armor before entering, then clear it
Manticore: Trial III | Find a World Raid nobody has touched yet and finish it in a single encounter (best to do with private raid)

## The Beastfelled Dungeon
This is the part that changes how the month plays. At ★7 and above , craft the Beastfelled Dungeon Key ⁠ in the Event Hub or Monumental Guild Hall , and any battle dungeon can be run as a Beastfelled Dungeon instead.
The crafting chain is short:
- Defeat event monsters.
- Collect Kerberos Fur ⁠ and Monoceros Horn ⁠ .
- Craft the Beastfelled Dungeon Key ⁠ .
- Run any dungeon as a Beastfelled Dungeon for the rest of the event.
Without the key you are waiting on the map to hand you event spawns. Best spot to frame before Beastfelled Dungeon are Beast Den dungeons.

## Monsters and Bosses
Everything in this event belongs to the Animal family, and the two Kin of Kerberos ⁠ carry Omnimancy : they are occasionally immune to damage from any basic element. Fighting them with a single-element build may waste turns.

## Items Worth Farming

## Lyncus’ Ring
Lyncus’ Ring ⁠ is the quest line reward and the single best reason to finish all eight trials. A ★8 Legendary accessory usable by all classes with a flat +90 to Attack, Magic, Defense, Resistance, HP, Mana and Dexterity . There is no drop-rate gamble here, you either clear the trials or you do not get it.

## Lyncus Paw
Lyncus Paw ⁠ is a ★10 Celestial augment for celestial weapons , applied at a Titan Workshop . It gives 4% Crit, 3% Ward and +5% Follower Act . Celestial-tier augments are rare enough that this alone justifies running Lyncus more than once.

## Lyncus’ Bracer
Lyncus’ Bracer ⁠ is a ★10 Legendary accessory: 8 Attack, 120 Mana, 6% Crit, 1% Ward . The crit number is the draw, and it stacks with the Paw if you are building for critical hits.

## Lyncus’ Bristle
Lyncus’ Bristle ⁠ is a ★10 Legendary armor adornment: -12 Attack, 2% Crit, 1% Ward, +3% Collateral Chance, +0.5% Avidity . Niche, but if your class has Collateral Damage or Avidity it is the only thing that raises both.

## Manticore Eye
Manticore Eye ⁠ is a ★8 Legendary armor adornment with 1% Ward and Follower/Summon Damage Taken -2% . Useful defensive tech against opposing follower builds, and it drops from the same Manticores you are already farming for hearts.

## Blackened Eye of Kerberos
Blackened Eye of Kerberos ⁠ is a ★9 Legendary weapon adornment from the Arisen Kin of Kerberos ⁠ : 10 Attack, 10 Magic, 10 Defense, 10 Resistance, 1% Ward, 4 Foresight, +2% Follower Act . It is a flat upgrade over the ★5 Dark Eye of Kerberos ⁠ , which gives the same Follower Act but only 2 Foresight and no stats.

## Beastfelled Archpath
Ten daily entries, alternating free and locked. Worth checking off because the final reward is where the bulk material payout sits.
Day | Quest | Reward
1 | Win a battle by exploiting an elemental weakness (1) | Bestial Potion ⁠ (2)
2 | Defeat Bosses (5) | Dragon Remains ⁠ (5)
3 | Defeat monsters (50) | Endless Treats ⁠ (3)
4 | Perform a Concurrence at an Oracle Temple (1) | Diluted Mnemonic ⁠ (3)
5 | Craft Potions (5) | Panacea ⁠ (10)
6 | Defeat enemies with a critical hit (3) | Sprite pack: Beastfelled I
7 | Upgrade equipment (5) | Appraiser’s Loupe ⁠ (3)
8 | Find and enter a Great Monument (1) | Sprite pack: Beastfelled II
9 | Complete dungeons (2) | Elysian Candle ⁠ (3)
10 | Defeat “Animal” monsters (10) | Sprite pack: Beastfelled III
Day 1 and Day 6 overlap with Kerberos: Trial I and Monoceros: Trial I, so you get those for free while doing the quest line.
The final reward for completing every entry includes an extra Activity Slot for the event window, 3 Monumental’s Summoning Scroll ⁠ , a Crest of the Felling ⁠ , 2 Astralseed ⁠ , 3 Appraiser’s Loupe ⁠ , 10 Skeleton Key ⁠ , 2 Diluted Mnemonic ⁠ , and 10 each of Kerberos Fur ⁠ , Manticore Heart ⁠ and Monoceros Horn ⁠ .
The unlocked Archpath adds more
Unlocking the Archpath adds a Player Profile Badge: Beastfelled , a Constellationseed ⁠ and a Questing License ⁠ on top of the free final reward. The 10 Manticore ⁠ Hearts alone cover the entire King Lyncus’ Legacy step, so finishing the Archpath can shortcut the hardest farm in the event.

## What Not to Miss
Ranked by how much you will regret skipping it:
- Find Eriq of Annwn first. Lyncus’ Ring ⁠ only comes from his trial line, and nothing else in the event replaces a +90-across-the-board accessory.
- Craft the Beastfelled Dungeon Key ⁠ at ★7+. It converts the whole month from map roulette into on-demand farming, and Kin of Kerberos ⁠ only spawn in Beast Dens.
- Finish the Archpath for the 10 Manticore Heart ⁠ . That is the King Lyncus’ Legacy step handed to you.
- Summon Turned King Lyncus ⁠ at ★10+ for the Beastfelled armor set and the Lyncus accessories. Every armor piece grants a follower or summon spell.
- Bank Kerberos Fur ⁠ and Monoceros Horn ⁠ early. Both crafting keys run on them, and the recipes leave with the event.

Kingsfall is the Avalon event. Nimue Lake monuments, eight fallen knights, two summoned super raids and the Apex gear.
“Great King Arthus, Fallen then Cursed. He still draws his sword for the Lady’s will.”
Kingsfall is the Avalon event. The mighty King Arthus has been cursed, and the whole month is built around felling him. You get 8 bosses , 10 raids , 11 followers .

## Quest line

The trials are split into three named sets that climb in tier: Kerberos ⁠ , Monoceros ⁠ , then Manticore ⁠ . Each one gates the next, and each one asks you to fight with something deliberately taken away.

## How Kingsfall works
The engine of the event is Memory of a King ⁠ , a ★6 Rare exotic that drops from every Kingsfall monster, every Fallen knight, and the Almighty Balin ⁠ , Arisen Balin ⁠ , Finesse ⁠ and The Last Hippogriff ⁠ raids. Memories are the event currency.
You spend them on Vial of Lakewater ⁠ at 25 memories each. Pouring a vial on the ground summons part of Nimue’s Lake to that spot for 24 hours . Nimue’s Lake is a 5 floor monument , and memories drop at the end of each monument room rather than from the individual battles inside it, so a clean clear pays out predictably.
That loop is the whole event: farm memories, plant a lake, run the lake, farm more memories.
The lake is designed not to pay for itself
The developers have tuned Nimue’s Lake repeatedly, cutting it to 4 floors then putting it back to 5, and dropping the vial price from 50 memories to 25. The stated goal is that the monument stays affordable and accessible but does not pay for itself. Expect to top up from world spawns and raids, not to farm lakes off lakes.
If you would rather not wander, the Kingsfall dungeon mode was added specifically to increase access to Kingsfall content. Any dungeon can be run in Kingsfall mode, which is the reliable way to get event enemies in front of you.

## The Round Table
Arthus’s knights show up in two grades. The ★6 Fallen versions are ordinary event bosses. The ★9 Cursed versions carry a superboss aura and are the ones that matter, because they drop the crests.
Name | Tier | Signature drops
Fallen Brunor ⁠ | ★6 | Quillon Dagger ⁠ , Memory of a King ⁠
Fallen Dagonet ⁠ | ★6 | Dagonet’s Epee ⁠ , Memory of a King ⁠
Fallen Lancelot ⁠ | ★6 | Lancelot’s Reach ⁠ , Memory of a King ⁠
Fallen Severause ⁠ | ★6 | Severause’s Halberd ⁠ , Memory of a King ⁠
Cursed Brunor ⁠ | ★9 | Crest of Brunor ⁠ , Brunor’s Cabochon ⁠ , Cursed Quillon Dagger ⁠
Cursed Dagonet ⁠ | ★9 | Crest of Dagonet ⁠ , Dagonet’s Cabochon ⁠ , Cursed Dagonet’s Epee ⁠
Cursed Lancelot ⁠ | ★9 | Crest of Lancelot ⁠ , Lancelot’s Cabochon ⁠ , Cursed Lancelot’s Reach ⁠
Cursed Severause ⁠ | ★9 | Crest of Severause ⁠ , Severause’s Cabochon ⁠ , Cursed Severause’s Halberd ⁠

## Cursed King Arthus
Crest of a Cursed King ⁠ is a ★10 Legendary field item that summons a projection of The Cursed King to your location. Kingsfall item crafting happens in the Event Hub , and the four knight crests are what the event gives you to work with.
Cursed King Arthus ⁠ is the headline fight at ★10 with 27,690,000 HP , which is by a wide margin the largest health pool in the event. He does not appear as a World, Kingdom or Other Realms raid. He only shows up when you summon him.
The two most important spells:
- King’s Reign ⁠ deals 2,000 to 4,000 damage to a single target and inflicts Cursed at 100% .
- Miasma III ⁠ rolls poison, blight, burning, paralysis, rot and toxic at 15% each, and stacks attack, magic, defense and resistance debuffs on top.

## Cursed Excalibur
Cursed Excalibur ⁠ is a ★10 two-handed sword for Warrior ⁠ classes: 350 Attack, 77 Magic, 44 Dexterity, 30% Ward, +15% Crit Damage, +10% Collateral Chance and +15% Collateral Damage , with 10 adornment slots . The cost is -150 HP and -150 Mana . Its off-hand ability, King’s Rage, hits every opponent for 1,100 power.
Ten adornment slots on a ★10 weapon is the real number here. Very little else in the game gives you that much room to build.

## Cursed Crown of the Fallen King
Cursed Crown of the Fallen King ⁠ is a Dark-element head piece for all classes with 9 adornment slots and a genuinely unusual stat line: +5% Collateral Damage, Manaflask Power, Summon Pacts, Follower Act, Apex Rate and Avidity , all on one item. It also grants Swordplay ⁠ to your followers and summons and gives Cursed at 5%.
Nothing else stacks that many different systems on a single slot. If you run followers, this is the head piece.

## The Last Hippogriff
The second summoned fight, and the one that matters most for endgame builds. The Last Hippogriff ⁠ is ★10 with 9,782,500 HP , summoned with the Windtamer’s Summoning Scroll ⁠ , a Monumental Guild scroll that recalls the story of Ruggiero, rider of the last Hippogriff.

## The fight
Deific Channel ⁠ is the swing point. It needs three turns in battle before it comes online, and once it lands the Hippogriff gets a large all-stat buff plus Ward for two turns.
Every Windtamer and Ruggiero armor piece grants Windswept immunity , so gear looted from this raid makes rerunning it noticeably easier.

## The other raids
Kingsfall runs seven more raids alongside the two summons, covering ★5 all the way to ★10.
Name | Tier | Availability

A Summoning Scroll of Merlin ⁠ is available in the Event Hub, which lets you summon a Merlin raid outside of Kingdoms. The generic Monumental’s Summoning Scroll ⁠ also works, since it summons raids belonging to the active world event.

## Finesse
Finesse ⁠ drops four weapons, one per archetype, and all four are worth a look:
- Finesse’s Greatsword ⁠ , Warrior ⁠ , 300 Attack with 8 adornment slots
- Finesse’s Katana ⁠ , melee, 290 Attack, 3% Crit, +15% Crit Damage, 30% status chance
- Finesse’s Shotel ⁠ , thief, 298 Attack, +20% damage to Ward and a 50% Defense down proc
- Finesse’s Enchanted Epee ⁠ , magic, 300 Magic, +20% arcane, dark, dragon and holy damage but -50% earthen, fire, lightning and water
The Epee is the interesting one. It is a hard commitment to advanced elements, and it is actively bad if your kit leans on the four basic ones.

## Followers
Eleven followers, and this is the deepest follower event in the calendar. Four of them come in both Cryptid and Steward grades sharing the same Bestial Bond, so the bond you want is available at two different tiers.
