Two more worth naming. Glatisant ⁠ , the Questing Beast, is a ★9 Dragon-family Cryptid with an attack rate of 50, which is the highest of any follower here by a wide margin, so it is the damage pick. Carman ⁠ is a ★7 Human Cryptid running Burn ⁠ , Decay ⁠ , Freeze ⁠ , Poison and Shock ⁠ with a debuff rate of 20, so she is the status-application pick.

## Items Worth Farming

## Hippogriff Eye
Hippogriff Eye ⁠ is a ★10 Legendary weapon adornment: 1% Ward and +2% Apex Power . Small on paper, but it is the only adornment that touches Apex at all, and with eight slots on the Lance you can run several.

## Cursed Jewel of Avalon
Cursed Jewel of Avalon ⁠ is a ★10 Famed weapon adornment with 12 Attack, 20 Dexterity, 1% Crit, 1% Ward, 1% Two-handed Power and 2% Follower Act . It is the generalist adornment of the event, and the Dexterity number is unusually high for the slot.

## The Cabochons
The four ★9 weapon adornments from the Cursed knights are cheap, specialised and easy to overlook:
Cabochon | Stats
Brunor’s Cabochon ⁠ | 20 Attack, 1% Crit Damage, 2% Status Effect Chance, +1 duration
Lancelot’s Cabochon ⁠ | 10 Attack, 25 HP, 1% Defense and Resistance Penetration, 1% 2H Power
Severause’s Cabochon ⁠ | 50 HP, 3% Follower Act, 3% Summon Pacts
Dagonet’s Cabochon ⁠ | 10 Attack, 10 Magic, 50 Mana, 1% Manaflask Charge, 1% Manaflask Power
Severause’s Cabochon ⁠ is the standout for follower builds, since Follower Act and Summon Pacts on the same adornment is not something you find often.

## What Not to Miss
Ranked by how much you will regret skipping it:
- Summon The Last Hippogriff ⁠ at ★10. The Apex set has no substitute anywhere else in the game, and the Windtamer Lance ⁠ alone justifies the scroll.
- Kill all four Cursed knights. Cursed Brunor ⁠ , Cursed Dagonet ⁠ , Cursed Lancelot ⁠ and Cursed Severause ⁠ hold the crests, and the cabochons are good adornments in their own right.
- Farm Fallen King Arthus ⁠ before the ★10. Avalon Crown ⁠ blocks Cursed, and King’s Reign ⁠ applies Cursed at 100%.
- Bank Avalon Ore ⁠ . Every Avalon raid drops it and every Avalon item upgrades with it, including the entire Cursed ★10 set. The recipes leave with the event.

## FAQ


=== Event: Of Giants and Titans - Stardrops, Mnemosyne, and Hyperion (https://playerecho.com/orna/event-of-giants-and-titans) ===
Of Giants and Titans is the tower event. Stardrop and Moondrop summons, two Mnemosyne tower modes, and the Hyperion super raid.
“The giants wanted to reach the stars. But the higher they climbed, and more dangerous the fall…”
Of Giants and Titans is the tower event, billed in-game as The Great Space Race . Jotunheim raised mountains, Olympia raised towers, and both sets of climbers fell out of the sky and left loot on the ground.
You get 6 bosses , 8 raids , 6 followers , two extra Tower of Olympia modes, and a super raid sitting on 21,300,000 HP .

## Quest line
Find Kaari of Jotunheim to start. She explains the Stardrops, then hands you off to Pythia , who turns bundles of them into followers. Four steps, no boss kills required, just currency.
- ★6 The Giants Find Celestial Stardrop ⁠ (1) Astralseed 25,000 orns 250,000 gold
The Giants
- Find Celestial Stardrop ⁠ (1)
- ★7 Memories of Giants Acquire Celestial Stardrop ⁠ (30) Jotunheim Mnemonic 40,000 orns 400,000 gold
Memories of Giants
- Acquire Celestial Stardrop ⁠ (30)
- ★8 The Titans Find Celestial Moondrop ⁠ (1) Astralseed 40,000 orns 400,000 gold
The Titans
- Find Celestial Moondrop ⁠ (1)
- ★10 Memories of Titans Acquire Celestial Moondrop ⁠ (20) Olympia Mnemonic 50,000 orns 500,000 gold
Memories of Titans
- Acquire Celestial Moondrop ⁠ (20)

## How the event works
There are two separate currencies and they do not mix.
Celestial Stardrop ⁠ is a ★6 Common exotic. Consuming one summons a Giant to your area. At ★6 and above you get them from Jotunheim’s fallen warriors in dungeons, and every Olympia raid drops them too.
Celestial Moondrop ⁠ is the ★8 Famed version and summons a Titan instead. It only comes off two monsters, the ★8 Fallen Moonchaser ⁠ and the ★10 Fallen Crescent-Fencer ⁠ , so it needs ★8 and above to farm at all.
Moondrops are the bottleneck, not Stardrops
Stardrops fall out of three monsters and all six Olympia raids. Moondrops come off exactly two monsters. If you want the Olympia Mnemonic ⁠ follower at the end of the quest line, that is 30 Moondrops from a two-monster pool.
The tower half of the event runs on a different item entirely. From ★4 and above , Memory Hunts pay out Fragment of Olympic Memory ⁠ , which the Monumental Guild turns into tower keys.
The Monumental Guild is also where you craft the Shrine of Asteria and the Tablet of Hyperion ⁠ from the drops you collect.

## Tiers at a glance
Almost every part of this event is gated separately, which catches people out.
Tier | What unlocks
★4+ | Fragment of Olympic Memory ⁠ from Memory Hunts, craft Mnemonic of Mnemosyne ⁠
★6+ | Collect Celestial Stardrop ⁠ in dungeons, summon Giants
★8+ | Collect Celestial Moondrop ⁠ in dungeons, summon Titans
★9+ | Extra fragments from completed Towers, craft Mnemosyne Puzzle Key ⁠
★10+ | Craft Tablet of Hyperion ⁠ for the super raid

## Monsters
All five turn up in Dungeons and Monuments . Every one of them is Rare rarity and carries Second Chance , so they occasionally survive a killing blow. Budget an extra turn.
Monster | Tier | Family | Drops
Fallen Starseeker ⁠ | ★6 | Human | Celestial Stardrop ⁠
Fallen Zwei-Caster ⁠ | ★7 | Human | Celestial Stardrop ⁠
Fallen Zwei-Fencer ⁠ | ★7 | Human | Celestial Stardrop ⁠
Fallen Moonchaser ⁠ | ★8 | Magical | Celestial Moondrop ⁠
Fallen Crescent-Fencer ⁠ | ★10 | Magical | Celestial Moondrop ⁠

## The Giants
Summoned with a Stardrop, all Common rarity, all Giant family. Every single one is weak to Lightning , so one loadout clears the entire set.
Name | Tier | Signature drops
Aegir ⁠ | ★6 | Stone ⁠ , Mountain Acorn ⁠ , Palm Tree Branch ⁠
Gymir ⁠ | ★7 | Wood ⁠ , Tree Nut ⁠ , Treecutter ⁠
Eistla ⁠ | ★8 | Stone ⁠ , Pistachio ⁠ , Willow Tree Branch ⁠
Arisen Aegir ⁠ | ★10 | Wood ⁠ , Mountain Acorn ⁠ , Arisen Palm Branch ⁠
Arisen Gymir ⁠ | ★10 | Wood ⁠ , Tree Nut ⁠ , Arisen Treecutter ⁠
Arisen Eistla ⁠ | ★10 | Stone ⁠ , Pistachio ⁠ , Arisen Willow Branch ⁠
The Arisen versions upgrade resistance into immunity
The ★6 to ★8 Giants resist Water, Fire and Earthen. The ★10 Arisen versions are immune to all three. Bring Lightning or bring nothing.
The Giant weapons are the reason to keep killing them. Arisen Treecutter ⁠ and Arisen Willow Branch ⁠ are ★10 two-handers with 12 adornment slots , which is more room than almost anything else at that tier.

## Mnemosyne and the two tower modes
This is the part of the event that veterans actually care about, and it has nothing to do with Stardrops.

## Mnemosyne’s Ascent
At ★4 and above , farm Fragment of Olympic Memory ⁠ from Memory Hunts and craft the ★4 Mnemonic of Mnemosyne ⁠ at the Monumental Guild. It opens Mnemosyne’s Ascent , a unique Tower of Olympia, and that tower is where the ★4 Shade of Mnemosyne ⁠ lives.
The Shade is a Cryptid with a stat line that reads like a typo: it is immune to physical, water, fire, lightning, earthen, dark, holy, dragon and arcane damage, plus poisoned, bleeding, burning, frozen, paralyzed, rot, asleep and blight.
It is a puzzle boss, so do kill it you do not need to inflict damage yourself. You need to drink Puzzle-maker’s Ambrosia ⁠ (which you collected in the tower) and it will damage the boss about 50% health.

## Shadows of the Titans
At ★9 and above , completed Towers of Olympia pay out extra fragments, which craft the ★9 Legendary Mnemosyne Puzzle Key ⁠ . That opens the Shadows of the Titans tower mode, home of the ★9 Titan Mnemosyne ⁠ . She is the only source of the five celestial augments below.
But before you can face her you can collect buffs by defeating the 5 other titans also found in this tower that will help you with the final fight:
- Titan Eos ⁠
- Titan Oceanus ⁠
- Titan Themis ⁠
- Titan Prometheus ⁠
- Titan Selene ⁠

## The Raids
Eight raids, and only one of them is worth planning your month around.
Name | Tier | Availability
Asteria of Olympia ⁠ | ★8 | Other Realms Raid, summoned with Celestial Moondrop ⁠
Atlas of Olympia ⁠ | ★8 | Other Realms Raid, summoned with Celestial Moondrop ⁠
Ophion of Olympia ⁠ | ★8 | Other Realms Raid, summoned with Celestial Moondrop ⁠
Zeus ⁠ | ★9 | Kingdom Raid (Kingdom Level 3) and Other Realms Raid
Arisen Asteria of Olympia ⁠ | ★10 | Other Realms Raid, summoned with Celestial Moondrop ⁠
Arisen Atlas of Olympia ⁠ | ★10 | Other Realms Raid, summoned with Celestial Moondrop ⁠
Arisen Ophion of Olympia ⁠ | ★10 | Other Realms Raid, summoned with Celestial Moondrop ⁠
Hyperion ⁠ | ★10 | Other Realms Raid, summoned with Tablet of Hyperion ⁠

## The Olympia trio (Tier 8 and Tier 10)
Asteria of Olympia ⁠ , Atlas of Olympia ⁠ and Ophion of Olympia ⁠ all sit at ★8 with 2,900,000 HP and split the Old Olympia armor set three ways, plus one weapon each.
The Arisen versions bump this to ★10 and the Arisen Olympia set. Arisen Asteria of Olympia ⁠ and Arisen Atlas of Olympia ⁠ run 3,650,000 HP , Arisen Ophion of Olympia ⁠ stays at 2,900,000 .
The Ophion line is the one summoner players want. Ophion Banner ⁠ and Arisen Ophion Banner ⁠ are restricted to Valhallan summoner classes and carry Summon Stats +20% and the unique effect to spread buffs to your summons .

## Zeus (Tier 9)
Zeus ⁠ sits at ★9 with 8,850,000 HP and is the only event raid you can find as a Kingdom Raid , at Kingdom Level 3.
He is immune to Lightning and is also immune to T.Res↓, Res↓↓ and Res↓ , so resistance shred does nothing here.
His drop table is the entire Olympia tower progression, Zeta through Omega, all six tiers of the Legendary set from ★5 to ★10. If you are short a single piece of Olympia gear, Zeus ⁠ is faster than climbing for it.

## Hyperion (Tier 10 Super Raid)
The headline. At ★10 and above , craft the ★10 Legendary Tablet of Hyperion ⁠ at the Monumental Guild to summon Hyperion ⁠ at 21,300,000 HP .
He is immune to Dark, Holy, Dragon and Arcane , so the advanced-element builds that carry most endgame content are dead weight. Bring basic elements.
Beyond the twelve Hyperion ⁠ pieces he drops Solarite ⁠ , Skyshard ⁠ , Tower Shard ⁠ and three mortars. Getting Tower Shards off a raid instead of a tower climb is on its own worth running him.

## The Hyperion Set
All twelve pieces are ★10 Rare exotics upgraded with Titanium ⁠ and Solarite ⁠ .
Weapon | Class | Core stats | Ability
Hyperion Sword ⁠ | Warrior ⁠ | 295 Attack, 36% Ward, +10% Collateral Damage | Atlas Stance (Off-hand) ⁠
Hyperion Staff ⁠ | Magic | 295 Magic, 36% Ward, +4% Follower Stats | Asteria Stance (Off-hand) ⁠
Hyperion Slingshot ⁠ | Thief ⁠ | 295 Attack, 36% Ward, +2% Mana Regen | Gymir Stance (Off-hand) ⁠
All three are two-handed with 8 adornment slots , 2% Crit , Monster Encounters +100% , View Distance +10% and a 2% chance to inflict Starstruck . Monster Encounters +100% on a ★10 weapon is the sleeper stat. Even if you never use the stance, this is a farming weapon you keep in the bag.
The stances are all trade-offs, not free power:
- Atlas Stance (Off-hand) ⁠ greatly increases damage and collateral chance, but your Ward drains heavily every turn.
- Asteria Stance (Off-hand) ⁠ increases magic power, costs a large chunk of defenses, and burns mana each turn.
- Gymir Stance (Off-hand) ⁠ increases attack power and damages you every turn.
The armor splits three ways by archetype. The warrior pieces (Helmet, Cuirass, Greaves) carry +3% Collateral Chance , the thief pieces (Cowl, Garb, Boots) carry Ward Recovery and Ward Start , and the magic pieces (Hood, Robe, Shoes) carry +1% Mana Regen . Every piece runs 8 adornment slots at max.

## Followers
Six, all Steward rarity, and none of them drop. You craft them from the two Mnemonics Pythia gives you at the end of the quest line.
Jotunheim Mnemonic ⁠ is ★8 Legendary and restores a Giant .
Olympia Mnemonic ⁠ is ★10 Legendary and restores a Titan .
Name | Tier | Family
Aegir ⁠ | ★6 | Giant, Steward
Gymir ⁠ | ★7 | Giant, Steward
Eistla ⁠ | ★8 | Giant, Steward
Asteria of Olympia ⁠ | ★10 | Titans, Steward
Atlas of Olympia ⁠ | ★10 | Titans, Steward
Ophion of Olympia ⁠ | ★10 | Titans, Steward
The two families run completely different bonds, and both cap at Bonded Ward 80% .
Giant bond , defensive:
- Level 1: Ward Start +2 turns, Earthen Res +20%
- Level 2: Bonded Ward (50%), Bonded Defense (8%), Bonded Resistance (8%), T. Def ↑ (7%), T. Res ↑ (2%)
- Level 3: Bonded Ward (80%), Bonded Defense (9%), Bonded Resistance (9%)
Titan bond , offensive:
- Level 1: +Arch-Alchemy, View Distance +2%
- Level 2: Bonded Ward (20%), Bonded Attack (8%), Bonded Magic (8%)
- Level 3: Bonded Ward (80%), Bonded Attack (30%), Bonded Magic (30%), +Ability: Build Tower ⁠
Bonded Attack and Magic at 30% each is the highest pair on this list, and Level 3 hands your follower Build Tower ⁠ on top.

## Items Worth Farming

## The Mnemosyne Augments
Five ★9 Celestial augments for celestial weapons, applied at a Titan Workshop . Four come off Titan Mnemosyne ⁠ in Shadows of the Titans.
Augment | Stats
Spine of Mnemosyne ⁠ | 12% Ward and nothing else
Shoulders of Mnemosyne ⁠ | 30 Attack, 30 Magic, 6% Ward, +5% Follower Stats, +5% Bestial Bond
Hands of Mnemosyne ⁠ | 30 Attack, 30 Magic, 3% Ward, +4% Tower Shards
Arms of Mnemosyne ⁠ | 80 Mana, +9% Manaflask Power, +7% Manaflask Charge
Feet of Mnemosyne ⁠ | 230 Dexterity , +10% Accuracy
Spine of Mnemosyne ⁠ gives a flat 12% Ward and literally nothing else, which is four times what the Hands version offers. You may want to stack them if you are building a celestial weapon around Ward.

## Heart of Mnemosyne
The fifth augment, and the exception: Heart of Mnemosyne ⁠ drops from the ★4 Shade of Mnemosyne ⁠ , not the ★9 Titan. 50 Attack, 50 Magic and Memory Hunting Rewards +2% .

## Band of Mnemosyne
Band of Mnemosyne ⁠ is a ★9 accessory: EXP Bonus +25%, Orn Bonus +25%, Two-handed Power +8%, Tower Shards +3%, Memory Hunting Rewards +2% and immunity to Stunned .
Two of these is a permanent 50% EXP and orn boost for the price of both accessory slots. If you are levelling anything, this is the best item in the event. Also, the Tower Shard ⁠ bonus is nice for tower farming.

## Mnemosyne’s Trinket
Mnemosyne’s Trinket ⁠ is a ★4 Famed consumable from the Shade. Use it during an unfinished Memory Hunt and your current step completes instantly and the hunt progresses.
Hoard these for the steps that ask you to walk somewhere inconvenient.

## Olympian Coin
Olympian Coin ⁠ is ★10 Famed and gives Tower Shards +100% for 48 hours . Do not burn one casually. Pop it before a tower session, not the moment it lands in your inventory.

## Of Giants and Titans Archpath
Ten daily entries, alternating free and locked. Several tick over from ordinary play, which makes this one of the cheaper Archpaths to finish.
Day | Quest | Reward
1 | Complete memory hunts (1) | Mnemonic of Mnemosyne ⁠ (1)
2 | Defeat Bosses (10) | Dragon Remains ⁠ (10)
3 | Defeat enemies with a critical hit (5) | Super EXP Potion ⁠ (1)
4 | Complete dungeons (2) | Sprite pack: Giants & Titans I
5 | Complete exploration dungeons (1) | Astralseed ⁠ (10)
6 | Upgrade equipment (25) | Galar’s Hammer ⁠ (2)
7 | Catch fish (20) | Jewel of Destruction ⁠ (1)
8 | Forfeit items to the Monumental Guild (40) | Svargan Fishing Line ⁠ (3)
9 | Defeat monsters (50) | Constellationseed ⁠ (1)
10 | Complete dungeons (10) | Sprite pack: Giants & Titans II (1)
The free final reward includes an extra Activity Slot, a Crest of the Felling ⁠ , 2 Mnemonic of Mnemosyne ⁠ , a Mnemosyne Puzzle Key ⁠ , 2 Monumental’s Summoning Scroll ⁠ , 5 Astralseed ⁠ , 3 Codexing Kit ⁠ , a Constellationseed ⁠ , and 3 each of Celestial Stardrop ⁠ and Celestial Moondrop ⁠ .
The unlocked Archpath is where the drops are
Unlocking with a Latchstone ⁠ raises the final reward to 2 Activity Slots, a Player Profile Badge, 5 Mnemonic of Mnemosyne ⁠ , 3 Mnemosyne Puzzle Key ⁠ , 5 Monumental’s Summoning Scroll ⁠ , 10 Astralseed ⁠ , 10 Codexing Kit ⁠ , the Titan Power Aura , and 20 each of Celestial Stardrop ⁠ and Celestial Moondrop ⁠ . Twenty Moondrops is two thirds of the Memories of Titans quest step handed to you.

## What Not to Miss
Ranked by how much you will regret skipping it:
- Bank Celestial Moondrop ⁠ from day one. Thirty of them off a two-monster pool is the longest farm in the event, and the Olympia Mnemonic ⁠ Titan followers are gated behind it.
- Farm Band of Mnemosyne ⁠ . +25% EXP and +25% orns each, two accessory slots, permanent. Nothing else here pays out for the rest of the year like that.
- Craft Mnemonic of Mnemosyne ⁠ at ★4+. Shade of Mnemosyne ⁠ drops Heart of Mnemosyne ⁠ and Mnemosyne’s Trinket ⁠ , and it is a ★4 fight.
- Run Titan Mnemosyne ⁠ for Spine of Mnemosyne ⁠ . 12% Ward on a celestial augment has no substitute.
- Summon Hyperion ⁠ at ★10+. Twelve pieces of gear plus Tower Shard ⁠ drops without climbing a tower.
- Kill Zeus ⁠ for missing Olympia pieces. He drops all six tiers, Zeta through Omega, and he is the only Kingdom Raid in the event.
- Finish the Archpath. The unlocked version alone covers most of a Mnemonic.

## FAQ


=== Event: Paths of Fomoria - Four Houses, Four Class Locks, and King Fomor (https://playerecho.com/orna/event-paths-of-fomoria) ===
Paths of Fomoria is the class-locked event. Four houses, twenty Lords, and gear that carries every house passive at once.
“A stone structure stands before you. Its construction is familiar, it is that of ancient Fomoria.”
Paths of Fomoria is the event that checks your class before it lets you play. Four Fomorian houses, four Epitaphs, and each one only talks to one archetype. Show up as the wrong build and the stone ignores you.
You get 20 Lords across the four houses, a King Fomor for each house, all of them at three grades, plus four followers and three summon spells.

## Quest line
Each house plants an Epitaph in the world, and you start the chain talking to the stone.

## The four houses
The house decides three things at once: who can start the chain, when the Lords spawn, and what is allowed to kill them. Every Lord carries a House ability that enforces the last one.
House | Starts the chain | Lords appear under | Kill them with
Wintara | Mage ⁠ classes | Falling snow | Magical abilities
Nocturna | Tamer and Summoner ⁠ | Nighttime | Followers or summons
Sumner | Warrior ⁠ classes | Sun | Physical might
Autumna | Thief ⁠ classes | Rain | Damage over time status effects
You can switch classes after accepting, but not out of the restriction
Once the Autumna quest is accepted you are free to go back to your preferred class. The kill condition does not go with it. You will still only be able to finish the required enemies with damage over time effects such as Burning .
Lords spawn on the map. The exception is King Fomor, who turns up in Monuments or wherever you use his Crest.

## Tiers at a glance
Every house runs the same three grades, so the tier gate is the same no matter which archetype you play.
Tier | What unlocks
★4 | The Lords, King Fomor, the Crests, and the base Fomor gear
★7 | Cernunnos ⁠ and Summon Cernunnos ⁠
★8 | Cursed Lords, Cursed King Fomor, Buggane ⁠
★10 | Arisen Cursed Lords, Arisen Cursed King Fomor, Glashtyn ⁠

## The Lords
Twenty of them, five per house. Each Lord drops exactly one piece of gear, and that piece carries their house’s passive. Collect a house’s five and you have the full set: head, torso, legs, off-hand, plus one extra torso.
One Lord per house is the leader and wears a superboss aura. Those are the fights worth planning around, because three of the four leaders also carry the upgraded version of their set piece.

## House of Wintara
Buff Duration +20% and Ally Effect Chance +15% on every piece, for Magic users. Expect Frost ⁠ , Tremor ⁠ and Winter Wind ⁠ coming back at you.
Name | Tier | Signature drop
Arisen Cursed Caturix of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Caturix’s Robe ⁠ , 287 Resistance, the best in the house
Arisen Cursed Esus of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Esus’s Pillar ⁠ , off-hand, 22% Ward
Arisen Cursed Imona of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Imona’s Slippers ⁠ , legs, immune to Drenched and Asleep
Arisen Cursed Lero of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Lero’s Hood ⁠ , head, immune to Frozen
Arisen Cursed Ogmios of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Ogmios’ Satin Robe ⁠ , torso, immune to Confused

## House of Nocturna
Summon Stats +8% , Follower Stats +8% and Follower/Summon Damage Taken -25% on every piece, for Valhallan and Summoner ⁠ classes. The Lords fight you with the same tools, so expect Summon Garm ⁠ , Summon Chimera ⁠ and Fright ⁠ .
Name | Tier | Signature drop
Arisen Cursed Atepomarus of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Atepomarus Mail ⁠ , 212/212 with +10% stats and -27% damage taken
Arisen Cursed Aengus Og of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Aengus Og’s Lute ⁠ , off-hand, 30% Ward
Arisen Cursed Artio of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Artio’s Hide ⁠ , torso, immune to Paralyzed
Arisen Cursed Belenus of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Belenus’s Boots ⁠ , legs, immune to Bleeding and Blight
Arisen Cursed Mullo of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Mullo’s Blinders ⁠ , head, immune to Blind

## House of Sumner
Multi-target Damage +15% and Chain Damage Chance +5% on every piece, for Warrior ⁠ classes. All five run the Omnistrike ⁠ , Osmostrike ⁠ and X Slash ⁠ line, and all five trade Resistance away at -100 for 262 Defense.
Name | Tier | Signature drop
Arisen Cursed Caticatona of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Caticatona Cuirass ⁠ , torso, immune to Bleeding
Arisen Cursed Bormana of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Bormana’s Mantle ⁠ , torso, immune to Frozen
Arisen Cursed Eostre of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Eostre’s Circlet ⁠ , head, immune to Stunned
Arisen Cursed Gobannos of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Gobannos’ Heavy Boots ⁠ , legs, immune to Petrified
Arisen Cursed Goibhniu of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Goibhniu Armguard ⁠ , off-hand, 26% Ward
Caticatona is the leader here, but her Cuirass is statistically identical to Bormana’s Mantle ⁠ . Sumner is the one house where killing the superboss buys you nothing extra.

## House of Autumna
Effect Damage +100% and Status Effect Chance +20% with +1 turn duration on every piece, for Thief ⁠ classes. This is the strongest house passive in the event and the one most builds actually want.
Name | Tier | Signature drop
Arisen Cursed Sequana of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Sequana’s Chestpiece ⁠ , 257/257 and Effect Damage +200%
Arisen Cursed Clota of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Clota’s Garb ⁠ , torso, 220/220, immune to Poisoned
Arisen Cursed Damona of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Damona’s Shield ⁠ , off-hand, 24% Ward
Arisen Cursed Niskus of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Niskus’ Boots ⁠ , legs, 245/245, immune to Burning
Arisen Cursed Sabrina of Fomoria ⁠ | ★10 | Arisen Cursed Sabrina’s Cowl ⁠ , head, 245/245, immune to Blight
Sequana's Chestpiece is the single best drop in the event
Double the Effect Damage of every other Autumna piece, the highest defenses in the house at 257/257 , 4% Ward , immunity to both Burning and Frozen, and no -40 Mana penalty. Every other Autumna torso and leg piece pays that mana cost. Hers does not.

## King Fomor and the Crests
Each house has its own Crest , a ★4 Famed item described as proof of trials complete. Use it and a projection of that house’s King Fomor appears at your location. All four Crests are useable by all classes , which is the one part of this event that does not care what you are wearing.
King Fomor also spawns in Monuments without a Crest.
Crest | Summons
Crest of Wintara ⁠ | King Fomor, Wintara ⁠
Crest of Nocturna ⁠ | King Fomor, Nocturna ⁠
Crest of Sumner ⁠ | King Fomor, Sumner ⁠
Crest of Autumna ⁠ | King Fomor, Autumna ⁠
All twelve King Fomor variants carry the superboss aura. Unlike the Lords, they have no House ability, so nothing restricts how you kill them.

## The Fomor set
King Fomor drops four pieces: Fomor’s Crown ⁠ , Fomoria Plated Robe ⁠ , Fomoria Plated Boots ⁠ and his house’s Crested Shield . Each comes in a Warrior ⁠ , Mage ⁠ , Thief ⁠ and Valhallan/ Summoner ⁠ cut, and each scales through ★4, ★8 and ★10.
Every piece carries all four house passives at once , which nothing else in the event does:
- Multi-target Damage +5% and Chain Damage Chance +5% , from Sumner
- Buff Duration +5% and Ally Effect Chance +5% , from Wintara
- Effect Damage +25% and Status Effect Chance +21% with +1 turn , from Autumna
- Summon Stats +8% , Follower Stats +8% and Follower/Summon Damage Taken -10% , from Nocturna
The crown, robe and boots run 4 adornment slots . The shields drop to 2 slots and +2% summon and follower stats, but pay it back in Ward.

## Monsters, summons and followers
Three beasts show up in all four houses. Each one exists as follower and summoner spell.

## The summon spells
You buy all three from Arcanists or the Monumental Guild . None of them drop.
Spell | Tier
Summon Cernunnos ⁠ | ★7
Summon Buggane ⁠ | ★8
Summon Glashtyn ⁠ | ★10

## The followers
Four Cryptid followers, all Animal family, and the two bonds do completely different jobs.
Name | Tier | Role
Cernunnos ⁠ | ★7 | Buff rate 8, the four Songs plus Holy Light ⁠
Glashtyn ⁠ | ★7 | Debuff rate 20, Dark Sigil ⁠ and Casted Shadows ⁠
Arisen Cernunnos ⁠ | ★10 | Buff rate 7, the four Sonatas plus Holy Light II ⁠
Arisen Glashtyn ⁠ | ★10 | Debuff rate 20, adds Casted Shadowga ⁠
The ★7 and ★10 versions share the same bond, so the Arisen upgrade buys you spell tier and stats, not a better bond.

## Items Worth Farming

## Arisen Cursed Sequana’s Chestpiece
Arisen Cursed Sequana’s Chestpiece ⁠ : 257 Defense, 257 Resistance, 4% Ward, Effect Damage +200%, Status Effect Chance +20% with +1 turn duration, 4 adornment slots , immune to Burning and Frozen. If you run status damage, this is the event.

## Arisen Cursed Atepomarus Mail
Arisen Cursed Atepomarus Mail ⁠ : 212/212, 3% Ward, Summon Stats +10%, Follower Stats +10%, Follower/Summon Damage Taken -27% , immune to Doom ⁠ and Confused. It beats the other four Nocturna pieces on every line.

## Arisen Cursed Aengus Og’s Lute
Arisen Cursed Aengus Og’s Lute ⁠ : 75 Attack, 75 Magic, 30% Ward , the full Nocturna passive, and immunity to Lulled and Asleep . Very little else gives you 30% Ward on an off-hand and still puts attack and magic on it.

## Arisen Cursed Damona’s Shield
Arisen Cursed Damona’s Shield ⁠ : 115/115, 24% Ward , the standard Autumna Effect Damage +100% , plus immunity to Paralyzed and Rot . Run it with Sequana’s Chestpiece ⁠ so the shield covers your Ward while the chest carries the bigger multiplier.

## What Not to Miss
Ranked by how much you will regret skipping it:
- Kill the house leader. Arisen Cursed Sequana of Fomoria ⁠ , Arisen Cursed Atepomarus of Fomoria ⁠ and Arisen Cursed Caturix of Fomoria ⁠ all drop upgraded versions of their set piece. Only Sumner’s leader does not.
- Bank the Crests. A Crest of Autumna ⁠ or its siblings is an on-demand King Fomor at your feet, which beats waiting on a Monument spawn.
- Take the ★10 Fomor set even off-archetype. All four house passives on one slot, with 4 adornment slots on the crown, robe and boots.
- Buy the summon spells. They costs gold or Monumental Guild proofs.

## FAQ


=== Event: Phoenixrise - Every Phoenix, Raid, and Ashen Drop (https://playerecho.com/orna/event-phoenixrise) ===
Phoenixrise is the community event. Stop the ritual, farm Lost Ember, and take the 35% Ward shield off the Ashen Phoenix.
Phoenixrise is the event where the whole server is on one quest. An ancient ritual is running, and the community either stops it or it doesn’t. That is what makes this one worth logging in for even if you do not care about the gear, because the community quest pays a daily bonus while it is active.

## Quest line
At ★2 and above , find Asherah . She explains what the ritual is and what can be done to stop it. That is the entry point, and there is no way into the event content around her.
When the community successfully stops the ritual, the Phoenixrise Community Quest pays out to everyone. It also carries a daily bonus for completing it, which is why this event is one of the better months for experience, orns and gold on top of the gear.
Quest steps are not published anywhere reliable
Unlike Wyrmhunt or Paths of Fomoria, the individual Phoenixrise quest steps and their per-step rewards are not in the public codex. Open your quest log in game and read the current step there rather than trusting a step list from a previous run of the event.

## The ritual is the mechanic
Two ★2 spells define this whole event, and both belong to the enemies, not to you.
Phoenix’s Pledge ⁠ marks one target as a Phoenix ⁠ Pledge and forces them into the ritual of Phoenixrise. It lands at a 100% application rate , so there is no dodging the mark.
Phoenixrise Ritual ⁠ is a spell that hits harder the higher the tier you belong to.
Phoenixrise Ritual scales with your tier, not theirs
A ★2 spell that reads damage off the target’s tier is a ★2 spell that hits an endgame character hardest. ★10 players take the most from it, cast by monsters as low as Phoenix Pledgeling ⁠ .

## Tiers at a glance
Tier | What unlocks
★2 | Asherah’s quest line, Phoenix Pledgeling ⁠ , Lost Ember ⁠
★5 | Phoenix Halfling ⁠ , Ascended Pledge ⁠ , Ember-Plumed set, Phoenixsong ⁠
★7 | Phoenix ⁠ raid, High Phoenician gear, High Phoenixsong ⁠
★8 | Phoenix ⁠ follower
★9 | Arisen Phoenix ⁠ , Fallen Ashen Phoenix ⁠ , Great Phoenician gear, Ash-Plumed set
★10 | Failed Phoenix ⁠ , Ashen Phoenician set

## The two raids
Name | Tier | HP and availability
Phoenix ⁠ | ★7 | 7,925,000 HP
Arisen Phoenix ⁠ | ★9 | 10,175,000 HP , the widest drop table in the event

## The world enemies
Everything below shows up in the world and in dungeons. All of them drop Lost Ember ⁠ .
Enemy | Tier | Notable
Phoenix Pledgeling ⁠ | ★2 | Peck ⁠ , Flame II ⁠ , Phoenixrise Ritual ⁠
Phoenix Halfling ⁠ | ★5 | Drops the base Phoenician armor pieces
Ascended Pledge ⁠ | ★5 | Boss, drops the full Ember-Plumed set and a Ruby ⁠
Great Phoenix Pledge ⁠ | ★9 | Drops Great Phoenician hood, robe and shoes
Fallen Ashen Phoenix ⁠ | ★9 | Superboss, drops the Ash-Plumed set and Ashen Ruby ⁠
Failed Phoenix ⁠ | ★10 | Same drop table as Great Phoenix Pledge ⁠
Rebirth means the fight is not over when the HP bar empties
Great Phoenix Pledge ⁠ , Failed Phoenix ⁠ and Phoenix Halfling ⁠ all carry Rebirth ⁠ . Budget mana and turns for a second round rather than dumping everything into the first kill.
The fastest way to see any of them is dungeons with Boss mode , Horde mode and Include event content enabled. Horde mode puts 4 to 5 enemies on a floor, which is 4 to 5 chances at Lost Ember ⁠ per floor.

## Lost Ember
Lost Ember ⁠ is the event currency. ★2 Rare , described as a warm spark taken from a Phoenix Pledgeling, and exchangeable at the Monumental Guild during Phoenixrise events.
It drops from all ten phoenix enemies. A ★2 monster and a ★10 monster both pay out, so there is no tier at which you stop farming this.
The exchange list closes with the event
The Monumental Guild exchange only exists during Phoenixrise. Check what it offers on day one and work backwards from the item you actually want, because embers still sitting in your inventory when the event ends buy nothing. The current exchange rates live in the Guild UI, not in the codex.

## Followers
Both phoenix followers are Animal family and share the exact same Bestial Bond : Accuracy +7% at level 1, then Bonded Attack 12% with Bonded Dexterity 7% at level 2 and 15% at level 3. The bond is not the reason to take the higher one.
Name | Tier | Rarity and kit
Phoenix ⁠ | ★8 | Cryptid, spell rate 5, buff rate 3, 3% protect. Tricut II ⁠ , Firestrike III ⁠ , Beaststrike ⁠ , Deific Channel ⁠ , Lusus Naturae ⁠
Ashen Phoenix ⁠ | ★10 | Steward, spell rate 5, buff rate 3, 3% protect. Deific Channel ⁠ , Emberseal II ⁠ , Emberstrike II ⁠ , Lusus Naturae III ⁠
Both followers are available for a limited time only, and both they and the summons are purchased during the event rather than dropped.

## Summons
Summon | Tier | Mana
Summon Great Phoenix Pledge ⁠ | ★9 | 350
Summon Failed Phoenix ⁠ | ★10 | 450
Both need 3 turns in battle before they land, which rules them out for anything that dies in two.

## Items Worth Farming

## Ashen Ruby
Ashen Ruby ⁠ : ★9 Legendary weapon jewel, all classes. 6 across Attack, Magic, Defense and Resistance, 15 HP , 15 Mana , 4 Dexterity , and Life Siphon +2% . It is one of few options to add life on hit to every class.
It only comes off Fallen Ashen Phoenix ⁠ , the ★9 superboss.
If you are below ★9 you may want to farm for Ruby ⁠ .

## Ashen Pinion
Ashen Pinion ⁠ : ★10 Legendary armor adornment, all classes. Dexterity +12 , Crit +4% , Ward +1% , and -40 to Attack, Magic, Defense and Resistance.
That is a deliberate trade, not a bad roll. If you are building crit and dexterity and can afford to give up the raw four, this is the adornment for it. Check what you already have slotted before you swap, because -40 across four stats is a real cost.

## What Not to Miss
Ranked by how much you will regret skipping it:
- Find Asherah at ★2 and keep the community quest ticking. The daily bonus is free experience, orns and gold for the entire month, and the community reward only pays if the ritual actually gets stopped.
- Kill Fallen Ashen Phoenix ⁠ when you find one. Superboss, and the only source of Ashen Ruby ⁠ .
- Buy the followers and summons while they are purchasable.
- Spend your Lost Ember ⁠ before the event ends. The Monumental Guild exchange goes with it.
- Camp Arisen Phoenix ⁠ . 34 drops covering three armor tiers and all three Phoenixsong ⁠ weapons, the widest table in the event.

## FAQ


=== Event: Thronemakers - Every Raid, Seal, and Dynasty Drop (https://playerecho.com/orna/event-thronemakers) ===
Thronemakers is the follower gear event. Three seals, one choice, and a 14 million HP Sphinx at the end of it.
Thronemakers is the event that pays your follower instead of you. Nearly every Aaru piece hands a skill to your follower or summon.

## Quest line
You meet the Aaru Storyteller at ★4 , and the official event post is specific about the other half of the requirement: reach level 75 before you go looking. The Storyteller guides the month and walks you through the origin story of the great kingdoms.
The tasks are the usual event mix: complete a battle using a new Thronemakers spell, collect potions and learn where they came from, and codex the new event raids.
Finish the line and you get the Tail of a Sphinx ⁠ , which is the reason to do the questline even if you never touch a raid. ★6 Legendary , all classes, 100 Mana , Item Drop Quality +1% and Luck +25% .
The new spell is a ★3 scroll
The Thronemakers spell the quest asks for is Sands of Aaru ⁠ , taught by the ★3 Legendary scroll Aaru’s Secret ⁠ . It costs 2 mana , has 1000 power , applies Blind at 10%, and uses either your magic or your attack stat depending on which is higher. Read the scroll before you go hunting for the quest step.
Individual quest steps are not in the public codex
The per step reward list for Thronemakers is not published anywhere reliable. Open your quest log in game and read the current step there instead of trusting a step list copied from an earlier run of the event.

## The seal choice
Four ★6 Rare seals exist, all sharing the same line: “Old seal of Aaru, marking a new land.” Three of them come off the Arisen raids, and those three are the ones the event actually asks you for.
Seal | From
Old Seal of Lyonesse ⁠ | Medjed ⁠ , Shade of Neith ⁠ , Arisen Thronemaker Neith ⁠
Old Seal of Avalon ⁠ | Medjed ⁠ , Shade of Ptah ⁠ , Arisen Thronemaker Ptah ⁠
Old Seal of Annwn ⁠ | Medjed ⁠ , Shade of Sekhmet ⁠ , Arisen Thronemaker Sekhmet ⁠
Old Seal of Elysium ⁠ | Medjed ⁠ , Shade of Osiris ⁠

## Scroll of Dynasty
Scroll of Dynasty ⁠ is a ★10 Legendary useable, all classes, described as a piece of literature crafted by the Monumental Guild that recalls the story of the Great Sphinx ⁠ and summons a projection of it when read.
That projection is the only route to the Dynasty armor set and Crook and Flail ⁠ . If you want the best gear in the event, this is the fork you take.

## Eastern Regalia
The other option is taking the seals to the Monumental Guild and rolling on Eastern Regalia ⁠ , the event weapon that removes your follower in exchange for raw power.
★10 Legendary two-handed sword, all classes, 9 adornment slots : 295 Attack , 295 Magic , 24 Defense, 24 Resistance, 95 HP, 95 Mana, Crit 5% , Ward 38% , and +25% damage while carrying no follower . It carries Cleave ⁠ . It also drops from Ramesses of the East ⁠ , so the Guild roll is a shortcut, not the only path.
Eastern Regalia is the opposite of the rest of the event
Every Aaru piece in Thronemakers exists to make your follower better. Eastern Regalia ⁠ and Eastern Mask ⁠ pay you to have no follower at all. Committing to the no-follower build means most of the event’s loot table stops applying to you, so decide before you burn seals, not after.

## Tiers at a glance
Tier | What unlocks
★3 | Sphinx Cub ⁠ follower, Aaru’s Secret ⁠
★4 | Aaru Storyteller quest line (level 75), Aaru Cobra ⁠ , Sands of Aaru ⁠
★6 | Thronemaker Neith ⁠ , the four Shades, the Eyes, the Old Seals, Tail of a Sphinx ⁠
★7 | Thronemaker Sekhmet ⁠ , Aaru Cobra ⁠ follower
★8 | Thronemaker Ptah ⁠ , Cursed Tail of a Sphinx ⁠
★10 | All four Arisen raids, Great Sphinx ⁠ , the Dynasty ⁠ set, Medjed ⁠

## The raids
Every base Thronemaker runs as a World Raid , a Kingdom Raid and an Other Realms Raid , so you get shots at them without waiting on your kingdom to organise anything.
Name | Tier | HP and drops
Thronemaker Neith ⁠ | ★6 | 2,055,000 HP , warrior gear and the Khopesh ⁠
Thronemaker Sekhmet ⁠ | ★7 | 2,730,000 HP , thief gear and Satis ⁠
Thronemaker Ptah ⁠ | ★8 | 6,540,000 HP , mage gear, no weapon
Arisen Thronemaker Sekhmet ⁠ | ★10 | 4,710,000 HP , Old Seal of Annwn ⁠
Arisen Thronemaker Ptah ⁠ | ★10 | 6,649,000 HP , Old Seal of Avalon ⁠
Arisen Thronemaker Neith ⁠ | ★10 | 9,420,000 HP , Old Seal of Lyonesse ⁠
Arisen Thronemaker Osiris ⁠ | ★10 | 9,615,000 HP , summoner gear and Final Decree ⁠
Great Sphinx ⁠ | ★10 | 14,219,000 HP , the entire Dynasty set

## The three siblings
Thronemaker Neith ⁠ at ★6 is the softest raid in the event at 2,055,000 HP . “A Goddess of wisdom, Neith migrated to the North.”
Thronemaker Sekhmet ⁠ at ★7 holds 2,730,000 HP . “A Goddess of destruction, Sekhmet migrated to the East.”
Thronemaker Ptah ⁠ at ★8 jumps to 6,540,000 HP , which is more than double Sekhmet for one extra tier. “A God of creation, Ptah migrated to the South.”

## The Arisen four
All four sit at ★10 and this is where the seals live.
Arisen Thronemaker Sekhmet ⁠ : “A Goddess of destruction, Sekhmet migrated to the East.”
Arisen Thronemaker Ptah ⁠ : “A God of creation, Ptah migrated to the South.”
Arisen Thronemaker Neith ⁠ : “A Goddess of wisdom, Neith migrated to the North.”
Arisen Thronemaker Osiris ⁠ is the odd one out. He drops no seal at all. What it drops instead is the only Valhallan and Summoner ⁠ gear in the event: Arisen Aaru Sandals ⁠ , Arisen Aaru Tunic ⁠ and Final Decree ⁠ .
There is no base tier Osiris raid
Neith, Sekhmet and Ptah each have a ★6 to ★8 version and a ★10 Arisen version. Osiris only exists as the Arisen raid. His low tier gear comes off Shade of Osiris ⁠ instead, which is a ★6 boss.

## Great Sphinx
Its kit borrows from the kingdoms the seals name: Curse ⁠ , Annwn Fury ⁠ , Anthem of Avalon ⁠ , Lyon’s Execution ⁠ , Lyon’s Mark ⁠ , King’s Reign ⁠ , Anthem of Lyonesse ⁠ , Aaru’s Blessing ⁠ , Arcane Strikes ⁠ IV and Verse III ⁠ .
Ten drops, and every one of them is worth having: the nine Dynasty armor pieces and Crook and Flail ⁠ .

## The Shades and the world enemies
The four Shades are ★6 event bosses and they are the cheapest source of Old Seals, the Eyes, and a set of side gear that never shows up in the raid tables.
Name | Tier | HP and drops
Shade of Neith ⁠ | ★6 | Aaru Shield ⁠ , Eye of Neith ⁠ , Old Seal of Lyonesse ⁠
Shade of Sekhmet ⁠ | ★6 | Aaru Dagger ⁠ , Eye of Sekhmet ⁠ , Old Seal of Annwn ⁠
Shade of Ptah ⁠ | ★6 | Aaru Circlet ⁠ , Eye of Ptah ⁠ , Old Seal of Avalon ⁠
Shade of Osiris ⁠ | ★6 | Heka ⁠ , Nekhakha ⁠ , Eye of Osiris ⁠ , Old Seal of Elysium ⁠
The Eyes are ★6 Rare armor adornments usable by all classes, and they are cheap stat glue:
- Eye of Neith ⁠ : Ward +1% , Ward Power +1%
- Eye of Sekhmet ⁠ : Attack +20 , Crit +2%
- Eye of Ptah ⁠ : Magic +8 , Manaflask Charge +1% , Manaflask Power +1%
- Eye of Osiris ⁠ : Follower Stats +1% , Follower Act +1% , Summon Stats +1%
Two items make event farming much less painful
Monumental’s Candle ⁠ is a ★4 Rare that gives +50% event encounters for 48 hours . Monumental’s Summoning Scroll ⁠ is a ★6 Legendary that summons raids belonging to the currently active world event, which is the on demand version of waiting for a kingdom raid.

## The Aaru gear
Every Aaru torso and leg piece grants a skill to your followers rather than to you. Every piece is Famed , has 4 adornment slots , and is immune to Cursed .
The three head pieces are a deliberate trap: they carry offense and enormous negative Defense and Resistance. Slot them only if you are already surviving comfortably.

All ★10 , all Famed , all 4 adornment slots except the Sandals, which get 5 .

## The Dynasty set
Great Sphinx ⁠ loot, and the only gear in the event that gives the skill to you . Nine pieces, all ★10 Famed , all 6 adornment slots , all immune to Cursed , all with Ward 2% .
Class | Head | Torso | Legs | Grants
Warrior ⁠ | Helmet of Dynasty ⁠ | Cuirass of Dynasty ⁠ | Greaves of Dynasty ⁠ | Walls of Aaru ⁠
Thief ⁠ | Cowl of Dynasty ⁠ | Chestguard of Dynasty ⁠ | Clogs of Dynasty ⁠ | Curse of Sekhmet ⁠
Magic | Hood of Dynasty ⁠ | Houserobe of Dynasty ⁠ | Footpads of Dynasty ⁠ | Gods of Aaru ⁠
The three skills are the reason to chase the set:
- Walls of Aaru ⁠ : ★10 , 240 mana , self. Recovers, grants Ward for 6 turns and pushes damage absorption to 100% . Needs 2 turns in battle.
- Curse of Sekhmet ⁠ : ★10 , 140 mana . The target cannot heal or revive, and takes damage every turn.
- Gods of Aaru ⁠ : ★10 , 320 mana , self. T. All ↑↑↑ (100%) plus Ward. Needs 2 turns in battle.
All three pieces in a line grant the same skill, so the head, torso and legs of your class row are chasing one spell between them.

## Followers
Four followers, all Cryptid , all described as available for a limited time only, and all four share the exact same Bestial Bond : EXP +3% and Orn +3% at level 1, then Bonded HP (80%) at levels 2 and 3. Pick on kit alone, the bond is identical.
Name | Tier | Rates and kit
Sphinx Cub ⁠ | ★3
Aaru Cobra ⁠ | ★7
Aaru Viper ⁠ | ★10
Sphinx Kit ⁠ | ★10

## Summons
Summon Sphinx Kit ⁠ costs 300 mana , summons at 100% , and needs 3 turns in battle , so it is useless in anything that ends fast.

## Items Worth Farming

## Crook and Flail
Crook and Flail ⁠ : ★10 Famed , Axes and Hammers, all classes, 8 adornment slots . 260 Attack , 260 Magic , 12 across Defense, Resistance and Dexterity, Crit 13% , Ward 34% and Follower Act +10% .
It carries Cleave ⁠ , applies Burning, Frozen, Paralyzed and Rot at 5% each, grants T. All ↑ (5%) , and is immune to Cursed , Asleep and Petrified . Equal attack and magic on an all class weapon with 34% Ward is the best single item in the event.

## Final Decree
Final Decree ⁠ : ★10 Legendary Archistaff for Valhallan and Summoner ⁠ classes, 9 adornment slots . 205 Magic , 50 Attack, 55 Mana, Ward 22% , Summon Pacts +60% and Chain Damage Chance +15% , with Summon Kheper Swarm ⁠ attached.
+60% Summon Pacts is the number here. It drops from Arisen Thronemaker Osiris ⁠ and, outside the event, from The Fool.

## Tail of a Sphinx
Tail of a Sphinx ⁠ : ★6 Legendary accessory, all classes. 100 Mana , Item Drop Quality +1% , Luck +25% . Free from the questline, so there is no excuse.
The ★8 Cursed Tail of a Sphinx ⁠ doubles down: Item Drop Quality +2% and Luck +50% , paid for with -200 HP and -200 Mana . Farming accessory, not a fighting one.

## What Not to Miss
Ranked by how much you will regret skipping it:
- Do the questline for the Tail of a Sphinx ⁠ . ★4 and level 75, and +25% Luck improves everything else you farm this month.
- Farm Arisen Thronemaker Sekhmet ⁠ for seals. 4,710,000 HP is half of what Neith asks for the same class of drop.
- Kill Great Sphinx ⁠ for Crook and Flail ⁠ . All classes, 8 slots, 34% Ward, equal attack and magic.
- Take the Arisen Aaru Sandals ⁠ if you summon. Reraise ⁠ on a summon changes how long fights last.
- Grab the Eyes off the Shades. ★6 bosses, four adornments, and Eye of Osiris ⁠ is one of the few items that boosts follower and summon stats at once.
- Light a Monumental’s Candle ⁠ . +50% event encounters for 48 hours applies to everything above.

=== Event: Wyrmhunt 2026 - Every Dragon, Item, and Reward (https://playerecho.com/orna/event-wyrmhunt) ===
Wyrmhunt runs included 13 Great Dragons, the raids, the crafting items, and the Tower of Yelbeghen.
“Long ago, this land was created by the Great Dragons. Twice a year, they return to see it.”
That is a full month of Great Dragons, and it only comes around about twice a year. You can do 3 raids , 13 world bosses and get 12 followers for this event.

Most event bosses show up just from traveling the world of Orna. If you would rather not leave it to the map, some of them may also show up in dungeons (be sure to include event content) and monuments.

Every one of them belongs to the Dragons family and every one carries the Great Dragon ability: many skills and spells they cast have a chance to inflict Drakeblight ⁠ .

Agathodaemon ⁠ | ★7
Zaltys, Friendly One ⁠ | ★7
Bashe, Hungry One ⁠ | ★9
Draconian Gorynych ⁠ | ★10
Gorynych, Son of Mountains ⁠ | ★10
Peluda, Poison Quilled ⁠ | ★10
Ymir Amphiptere ⁠ | ★10
Illuyanka, Made of Stone ⁠ | ★8
Quetzalcoatl, Feathered One ⁠ | ★9

## Items Worth Farming

## Yelmogus Greatstaff
Yelmogus Greatstaff ⁠ have a skill bind to it that is one of the best AOE abilities (if not the best) in the whole game. It is used for many different classes for dungeons/tower builds.

## Jewel of Creation
Jewel of Creation ⁠ is an armor adornment, usable by all classes. Dropped by Ymir Amphiptere ⁠ only. It is one of few adornments offering +% ward, so it is worth farming.
Attack: 6, Magic: 6, Defense: 12, Resistance: 12, HP: 18, Mana: 18, Ward: 1%

## Dragon Head
Dragon Head ⁠ is the event main currency and most of the drangon bosses drop it. I is used for many crafting recipes. he Monumental Guild Hall recipes disappear when Wyrmhunt ends, so a head you did not pick up is a craft you do not get.

## Drake Blood
Drake Blood ⁠ cures any elemental blight and is also dropped by many of the dragon bosses.

## Towers of Yelbeghen
At ★9 and above , craft the Yelbeghen Tower Key ⁠ at the Monumental Guild using the heads you have collected from the Great Dragons. The key converts any Tower of Olympia run into a Tower of Yelbeghen instead.
For endgame players this is the most valuable thing in the event, and it runs entirely on Dragon Head ⁠ supply. Decide early in the month whether you are going for tower keys, because heads spent on other recipes are heads you will not have when you want a key.

## Wyrmhunt Archpath
Ten daily entries, alternating free and locked. Most of them are element-spell quests you can clear on any event monster, so the whole path is cheap to tick off while you farm.
Day | Quest | Reward
1 | Defeat “Dragon” monsters (10) | Codexing Kit ⁠ (25)
2 | Complete Dungeons (2) | Dragon Remains ⁠ (10)
3 | Enchant an item at an Alchemist (5) | Galar’s Hammer ⁠
4 | Finish a battle by using a spell of the Fire element (5) | Vulcan’s Brew ⁠ (5)
5 | Finish a battle by using a spell of the Lightning element (5) | Great Dowsing Rod ⁠ (5)
6 | Finish a battle by using a spell of the Water element (5) | Dragon Remains ⁠ (5)
7 | Finish a battle by using a spell of the Earth element (5) | Jewel of Destruction ⁠ (5)
8 | Finish a battle by using a spell of the Dragon element (20) | Codexing Kit ⁠ (5)
9 | Defeat Lotan, Coiled One ⁠ (5) | Beast’s Blood ⁠ (10)
10 | Defeat “Dragon” monsters (50) | Crest of the Felling ⁠ (1)
Day 9 overlaps with the first step of Dragonhunter Brin’s quest line, and Days 1 and 10 tick over on their own while you hunt the Great Dragons.
Completing every entry gives a Crest of the Felling ⁠ , 5 Codexing Kit ⁠ , 2 Diluted Mnemonic ⁠ , 5 Summoning Scroll ⁠ , and 2 each of Head of Lotan ⁠ , Head of Vritra ⁠ , Head of Agathodaemon ⁠ , Head of Zaltys ⁠ , Head of Illuyanka ⁠ , Head of Beithir ⁠ and Head of Bashe ⁠ .
The unlocked Archpath adds more
Unlocking the Archpath with a Latchstone ⁠ upgrades the final reward to a Player Profile Badge: Wyrmhunt , a Crest of the Felling ⁠ , 50 Codexing Kit ⁠ , 5 Diluted Mnemonic ⁠ , 10 Dragon Remains ⁠ , 10 Summoning Scroll ⁠ , 10 Monumental’s Summoning Scroll ⁠ , 10 of each of the seven dragon heads, and the Wyrmfire Aura appearance.

## What Not to Miss
Ranked by how much you will regret skipping it:
- Talk to Dragonhunter Brin at ★5+. Everything else follows from the quest line.
- Bank Dragon Heads. They only convert at the Monumental Guild Hall during Wyrmhunt. At ★9+ they become Yelbeghen Tower Key ⁠ .
- Complete Codex entries as you go. No completed entry means no Bestiary access and no follower.
- Chase Ymir Amphiptere ⁠ for the Jewel of Creation ⁠ . A ★10 Legendary adornment with ward is not something you pick up without this event.
- Run Arisen Quetzalcoatl ⁠ for the Fallen Sky set while it is available as a World Raid.
- Grab a good Yelmogus Greatstaff ⁠ as it is one of the best AOE weapons ingame.
- Finish the Archpath. The final reward hands you a stack of dragon heads outright, on top of the Crest of the Felling ⁠ .
Twice a year only
Wyrmhunt returns roughly every six months. Anything you leave unfinished have to wait half a year.

## FAQ


=== Every Orna Event: Guides, Tier Gates, and Rewards (https://playerecho.com/orna/events) ===
Index of every Orna and Hero of Aethric event guide on this site, plus the shared format every event runs on.
Orna runs month long events on rotation, and each one hides its best gear behind a different gate. Below are the seven with full guides here, plus the format they all share so a new event is never a fresh start.

## Every event guide on this site
Event | Tier gates
Wyrmhunt | ★5 to ★10
Beastfelled | ★3 to ★10
Kingsfall | ★5 to ★10
Of Giants and Titans | ★4 to ★10
Paths of Fomoria | ★4 to ★10
Phoenixrise | ★2 to ★10
Thronemakers | ★3 to ★10

## How Orna events work
The wrapper barely changes between events. Learn it once and each new one is a reskin with different tier gates.

## The quest line is the gate
Events open with an NPC quest line and the rest of the month hangs off it. Skip it and you lock yourself out of the followers and the crafting recipes, not just the orns.
Two of the four hand you the good stuff purely through quest steps: Of Giants and Titans gives both its follower Mnemonics as quest rewards from Pythia , and Beastfelled gates Lyncus’ Ring ⁠ behind the Eriq of Annwn trial line. Nothing else in either event replaces those.

## Getting event enemies to spawn
Waiting on the world map is the slow way. Most event pushes its enemies into dungeons instead:
- Run dungeons with Boss mode , Horde mode and Include event content enabled. Horde mode spawns 4 to 5 enemies per floor, which is why it is the event farming pick.
- You can pick which specific events to include from the dungeon UI. Exclude the ones you are not farming so the spawn pool goes to the one you are.
- Some events add their own dungeon mode. Kingsfall dungeon mode was added specifically to increase access to event content, and the Beastfelled Dungeon Key ⁠ at ★7+ turns any battle dungeon into a Beastfelled Dungeon.

## The Archpath is 10 days of free material
Events ship a 10 entry Archpath , alternating free and locked entries. A Latchstone ⁠ unlocks the paid half and upgrades the final reward, usually with a profile badge, an aura appearance, and a much bigger material dump.
Most daily entries tick over from ordinary play anyway: defeat bosses, complete dungeons, upgrade equipment.

## First time in an event? Do these

## Priority order for any event you have never played
- Find the quest NPC before anything else. Every event’s unique rewards sit behind the quest line.
- Check the tier table in the event guide. Most events gate their currency, their key and their super raid at three different tiers.
- Craft the dungeon key or enable the event dungeon mode. On demand spawns beat walking the map.
- Bank raw materials from day one. The bottleneck item is usually the one with the smallest drop pool.
- Complete Codex entries as you fight. No completed entry means no Bestiary access and no follower.
- Finish the Archpath. The final reward is the bulk payout, and it often shortcuts the worst farm in the event.

## FAQ


=== Orna Fishing Guide: Lines, Fish, Coral, and Fishing Hut Rewards (https://playerecho.com/orna/fishing) ===
How fishing works in Orna: getting started, lines, fish types and coral values, the Fishing Hut catalog, and Master Angler achievements.
Fishing in Orna runs off the Angler’s Guild. Head to any water source or fountain, use a fishing line, and you might pull up a fish, a random item, or a monster fight. Fish sell for Coral ⁠ at the Fishing Hut, the currency for gear and materials.

## Getting Started
Find a water source or fountain, open your inventory’s items section, and use any fishing line. Your character sits at a dock. Tap once for the casting bar, tap again to release. Fish drift across the screen in waves and hook automatically when they pass your bobber.
Higher-tier lines reduce the wait between bites. Let too many escape and the line snaps.
How to suspend your line

## Fishing Lines
Many lines are for sale at the Fishing Hut for coral and can be found in the astral tree. The Fishing Line ⁠ also shows up at regular shops and the Grand Market for 50 gold.
Line | Tier
Piece of String ⁠ | 1
Fishing Line ⁠ | 1
Svargan Fishing Line ⁠ | 2
Strong Fishing Line ⁠ | 3
Great Fishing Line ⁠ | 5
Mighty Fishing Line ⁠ | 7
Perfect Fishing Line ⁠ | 9
There is a daily cap of 500 fish , roughly 2.5 hours of straight fishing with a Perfect Fishing Line ⁠ .

## What You Can Catch
What comes up depends on the cast. Usually it is a fish, sold at the Fishing Hut for Coral ⁠ or used at the Herbalist for potions. Sometimes you get a random item at whatever tier, quality, and element the game decides. Occasionally a monster shows up instead from the Water Mob Spawn Table. Beat it for Coral ⁠ ; HP and mana reset before the next.

## Fish and Coral Values
There are 7 fish from ★1 to ★6. Heavier catches pay more coral. The floor weights below are fixed minimums.
Fish | Tier | Floor Weight | Coral ⁠ Range
Unremarkable Fish ⁠ | 1 | 1.9 g | 0-7
Azure Carp ⁠ | 1 | 9.5 g | 4-37
Emerald Trout ⁠ | 2 | 19.0 g | 19-101
Sea Bass ⁠ | 3 | 22.8 g | 36-260
Crimson Snapper ⁠ | 3 | 38.0 g | 61-396
Embered Snapper ⁠ | 5 | 26.6 g | 77-478
Balor Muskellunge ⁠ | 6 | 76.0 g | 276-1,935
The Balor Muskellunge ⁠ is worth chasing: a heavy one can fetch nearly 1,935 coral per catch.

## The Fishing Hut
The Fishing Hut has a permanent stock of lines, hats, and a handful of other items:
Item | Tier | Price

Fishing Line ⁠ | 1 | 10 coral
Strong Fishing Line ⁠ | 3 | 20 coral
Great Fishing Line ⁠ | 5 | 50 coral
Mighty Fishing Line ⁠ | 7 | 100 coral
Perfect Fishing Line ⁠ | 9 | 500 coral
Fishing Hat ⁠ | 3 | 5,000 coral
Great Fishing Hat ⁠ | 6 | 10,000 coral
Summoning Scroll ⁠ | 7 | 500 coral
Diluted Mnemonic ⁠ | 4 | 500 coral
Fisher Sprite Pack | 1 | 5,000 coral
Svargan Sprite Pack | 1 | 25,000 coral
Materials also rotate through the Fishing Hut and span every tier, from 2 coral for ★1 basics like Iron ⁠ or Wood ⁠ up to 21 coral for top-tier materials like Orichalcum ⁠ or Lyonite ⁠ .

=== Follower Guide by Tier and Activity (https://playerecho.com/orna/follower-guide) ===
Which Orna followers are worth buying, how Bestial Bond works, and which pets to use for raids, farming, dungeons, and more.
Followers fight alongside you in every combat encounter (except for Summoner ⁠ ). They scale to your level automatically, cost Gold or Orns to buy, and can provide stat bonuses through the Bestial Bond system if you have the right class or trait.

## What followers do
Every follower acts on its own each turn. Depending on its stat profile, it will attack, cast spells, buff you, debuff enemies, or heal you. You do not control which action it takes (at least not directly). The codex lists each follower’s rates for attack, spells, heals, buffs, debuffs, and protect chance.
Protect chance is the percentage chance the follower takes a hit for you. A few followers have notably high protect values ( 12% or more), which makes them useful in content where high damage hits are a problem.
Followers scale their stats to match your current player stats, so also lower followers may stay relevant but higher followers have better skills and may outperform lower once.
Followers do not:
- Work with the Summoner ⁠ classline. The Attuner ⁠ , Summoner ⁠ , and Grand Summoner ⁠ cannot tame followers at all.
- Stay automatically if you buy a replacement without storage. Buying a new follower removes your current one unless you have a Keep, Fortress, or Castle. So be sure to build one before switching followers.

## How to get followers
Followers are bought from Bestiary buildings. World Bestiaries (randomly placed on the map) sell up to ★3 followers only. To access higher tiers, you need your own Bestiary.
The Bestiary unlocks at Level 25 and costs 250,000 Gold , 25 Wood ⁠ , and 25 Hide ⁠ . Upgrading your Bestiary refreshes its stock and opens higher tiers.
Cost by tier:
Tier | Currency
1-7 | Gold
8+ | Orns
Limited-time event followers appear in Bestiaries only during their event window. The reliable second chance is the Friends and Family event, which runs for roughly three days around Christmas each year. During that event, all that year’s seasonal followers appear in Bestiaries simultaneously.

## Bestial Bond
Bestial Bond is a system that grants you stat bonuses based on which follower you have equipped. Each follower has three Bond levels with different bonuses at each tier.
Most classes get nothing from Bestial Bond. You fight alongside your follower, but the bond bonuses do not apply unless you meet one of these conditions:
- Valhallen classline ( Beowulf ⁠ and others of this class line): Full access to all three bond levels.
- Bestial Bond Amity trait: Bond Level +1.
- Tamer / Beastmaster specialization: Bond Level +1.
Bond Level 1 is often where the most useful bonuses sit, especially economy bonuses like Gold and Orn multipliers. Getting the Bestial Bond Amity is worth it even if you never plan to run the Valhallen line full-time.

## Beowulf Auriga / Bestla Auriga
These advanced Valhallen classes substantially amplify follower effectiveness:
- Enhanced Bestial Bond: all bond effects get an additional +12% multiplier
- Valhallan Dissonance II: +130% follower stats, +15% action frequency
- Auriga passive: follower +30% chance to act twice per turn , Follower/Summon AI +20%
- Valhallan Protection: +12% chance for the follower to shield you
- Both you and your follower gain +50% stats when you are at low HP
The follower acting twice per turn alone makes average followers competitive and top followers exceptional.

## Early followers (Tier 1-4)
The lowest tiers include Crow ⁠ , Dog ⁠ , Bat ⁠ and Bear ⁠ , Gnome ⁠ , Hellhound ⁠ , Imp ⁠ , Horse ⁠ , Great Bear ⁠ , and Living Armor ⁠ . At this stage, any follower is better than none. They cost only Gold and are a cheep upgrade for every class.
Do not spend time optimizing early followers. Buy whatever your Bestiary offers and replace them as you level up and higher tiers become available.

## Midgame followers (Tier 5-9)
These followers are meaningful upgrades and several remain useful well into endgame for specific activities.

## Arcane Slime (Tier 7)
Arcane Slime ⁠ is a steady magic attacker. Its Bestial Bond profile leans entirely toward magic damage. Solid for mage builds that want consistent magic-damage support.
Level | Bonus
BB1 | Elemental Weaknesses +15%
BB2 | T. Mag ↑ +7%, T. Mag ↑↑ 2%
BB3 | Bonded Magic +20%

## Camazotz (Tier 7)
Camazotz ⁠ with his Drain II ⁠ skill provides some HP sustain while it attacks. Strong for physical attack builds.
Level | Bonus
BB1 | Accuracy +7%
BB2 | Bonded Attack +12%, Bonded Dexterity +7%
BB3 | Bonded Attack +12%, Bonded Dexterity +15%

## Elysian Memories (Tier 7)
Elysian Memories ⁠ is A healing follower with a unique +20% Memory Hunting Rewards at BB1, accessible via the Bestial Bond Amity for any class. If you run Memory Hunts regularly, the reward bonus alone makes this follower pay for itself quickly. Combat stats are modest but adequate for midgame content.
Level | Bonus
BB1 | Bonded Attack +5%, Bonded Magic +5%, Memory Hunting Rewards +20%
BB2 | Bonded Attack +7%, Bonded Magic +7%
BB3 | Bonded Attack +8%, Bonded Magic +8%

## Coral Reef (Tier 7)
Coral Reef ⁠ is limited to the Terra’s Day and Terra’s Legacy: Natureblight events. The 12% protect chance is among the higher protect values on any follower. The protect chance makes it a defensive choice for content where surviving burst damage matters.
Level | Bonus
BB1 | Accuracy +7%
BB2 | Bonded Attack +12%, Bonded Dexterity +7%
BB3 | Bonded Attack +12%, Bonded Dexterity +15%

## Twilight Wisp (Tier 7)
Twilight Wisp ⁠ is a healing and support follower. Skills include Wisp’s Heal ⁠ , Wisp’s Heal II ⁠ , Faith ⁠ , and Nightmare ⁠ . The mix of heals and debuffs makes it a reliable general-purpose midgame pick before more specialized options are available.

## Pale Dragon (Tier 8)
Pale Dragon ⁠ is a survival-focused follower. Skills include Miracle ⁠ , Holystrike ⁠ , Dispel ⁠ , and Holy Light ⁠ , and it cures status effects. The combination of holy damage, healing, and status cleansing covers most defensive needs at ★8 without requiring a specialized build.

## Crimson Gazer (Tier 9)
Crimson Gazer ⁠ is an elemental DPS follower. Skills include Cataclysm II ⁠ , Fulmination II ⁠ , Glacier II ⁠ , and Inferno II ⁠ . Hitting all four elements means it stays relevant against almost any enemy type at ★9.

## Ancient Jinn (Tier 9)
Ancient Jinn ⁠ is a strong accessible option before you can get good ★10 followers. Skills include four elemental Fey spells ( Fey Flame IV ⁠ , Fey Frost IV ⁠ , Fey Spark IV ⁠ , Fey Tremor IV ⁠ ) and Jinn’s Talent ⁠ for buffing. Rare rarity keeps it more easy to get. Its BB3 profile is identical to ★10 Anubis ⁠ . If you access BB3, Ancient Jinn ⁠ closes most of the gap before you can get your hands onto Anubis ⁠ .
Level | Bonus
BB1 | +5% fire, water, earth, and lightning damage each
BB2 | Bonded HP (70%)
BB3 | Bonded Magic +20%, plus Wildfire II ⁠ , Chain Lightning II ⁠ , Natura II ⁠ , Winter Wind II ⁠ , and 4 Sigil variants

## Tier 9-10 followers
These are the followers most endgame players work toward. Cryptid rarity followers cost Orns.

## Cthulhu (Tier 10)
Cthulhu ⁠ is the economy leader. No other confirmed follower provides Gold and Orn bonuses at BB1, which is accessible via the Bestial Bond Amity for any class. In combat it casts Prism Wall ⁠ for defense and five elemental Fey spells ( Flame V ⁠ / Frost V ⁠ / Spark V ⁠ / Tremor V ⁠ plus Octoblast ⁠ ).
Level | Bonus
BB1 | Gold Bonus +14%, Orn Bonus +4%

BB3 | Bonded Attack +8%, Bonded Magic +8%, Bonded Defense +8%, Bonded Resistance +8%, T. All ↑ +3%

## Anubis (Tier 10)
Anubis ⁠ is one of the top non-event magic and elemental follower. Skills include Sigils for all four elements and Flame V ⁠ / Frost V ⁠ / Spark V ⁠ / Tremor VI ⁠ . The Debuff rate: 12 means it applies elemental Sigil debuffs more reliably than most followers.
Level | Bonus
BB1 | +5% fire, water, earth, and lightning damage each
BB2 | Bonded HP (70%)
BB3 | Bonded Magic +20%, plus Wildfire II ⁠ , Chain Lightning II ⁠ , Natura II ⁠ , Winter Wind II ⁠ , and 4 Sigil variants

## Chimera (Tier 10)
Chimera ⁠ ’s skills include Berserk ⁠ , Omnistrike III ⁠ , Blightstrike III ⁠ , and Wyvern Strikes ⁠ . The high Buff rate means Berserk ⁠ lands frequently, which can noticeably increase your damage output. This makes him a good follower for many builds.
Level | Bonus
BB1 | Accuracy +7%
BB2 | Bonded Attack +12%, Bonded Dexterity +7%
BB3 | Bonded Attack +12%, Bonded Dexterity +15%

## Fjalar (Tier 10)
Fjalar ⁠ is the non-event crit specialist and easy accessible due to Rare rarity. Skills include Aerial Barrage ⁠ , Quadcut II ⁠ , and Evasive Strikes ⁠ . Crit bonuses appear at all three BB levels. For crit-focused builds, Fjalar ⁠ is the first choice.

## Dark Pegasus (Tier 10)
Dark Pegasus ⁠ has dark-themed skills: Forbid ⁠ , Forbidden Art ⁠ , Casted Shadows II ⁠ , Gloomseal II ⁠ , and Gloomstrike II ⁠ . For dark/shadow builds the debuff kit pairs naturally with the bond numbers, but the bond values are not unique.
Level | Bonus

## Event followers worth prioritizing
All event followers are available only during their event window, plus the Christmas Friends and Family period.
Don't miss the Friends and Family window
This Christmas event is your best second-chance opportunity for event followers you missed. All that year’s seasonal pets appear in Bestiaries simultaneously for roughly three days.

## Arisen Spiritgarm (Tier 10, Ragnarok)
Arisen Spiritgarm ⁠ (ASG) is the magic nuke follower. Skills include Great Meditation ⁠ and four elemental Fey spells ( Cataclysm ⁠ , Fulmination ⁠ , Glacier ⁠ , Inferno ⁠ ). He is one of the highest damages followers you can find.
Chat Noir ⁠ (Hallowed event) is a visual reskin of Arisen Spiritgarm ⁠ with identical stats and Bestial Bond values. If you missed Spiritgarm ⁠ , watch for Chat Noir ⁠ during the Hallowed event.

Ashen Phoenix ⁠ is the premier buff follower at ★10. Its defining skill is Deific Channel ⁠ , which provides a temporary +100% boost to all stats . Every class that can benefit from a massive stat window.
